      Katame bhante ākāsassa dasa guṇā nibbāne anupaviṭṭhāti.
  Yathā mahārāja ākāso na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati
  na uppajjati appasayho acoragahaṇīyo anissito vihaṅgagamano
  nirāvaraṇo ananto evameva kho mahārāja nibbānaṃ na jāyati na
  jīyati na mīyati na cavati na uppajjati appasayhaṃ acoragahaṇīyaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 403]
  Anissitaṃ ariyagamanaṃ nirāvaraṇaṃ anantaṃ. Ime kho mahārāja
  ākāsassa dasa guṇā nibbāne anupaviṭṭhāti.
      Katame bhante maṇiratanassa tayo guṇā nibbāne anupaviṭṭhāti.
  Yathā mahārāja maṇiratanaṃ kāmadadaṃ hāsakaraṃ ujjotitatthakaraṃ evameva
  kho mahārāja nibbānaṃ kāmadadaṃ hāsakaraṃ ujjotitatthakaraṃ. Ime
  kho mahārāja maṇiratanassa tayo guṇā nibbāne anupaviṭṭhāti.
      Katame bhante lohitacandanassa tayo guṇā nibbāne
  anupaviṭṭhāti. Yathā mahārāja lohitacandanaṃ nāma dullabhaṃ asamasugandhaṃ
  sabbajanapasaṭṭhaṃ evameva kho mahārāja nibbānaṃ dullabhataraṃ
  asamasugandhaṃ ariyānaṃ pasaṭṭhaṃ. Ime kho mahārāja lohitacandanassa
  tayo guṇā nibbāne anupaviṭṭhāti.
      Katame bhante sappimaṇḍassa tayo guṇā nibbāne
  anupaviṭṭhāti. Yathā mahārāja sappimaṇḍo vaṇṇasampanno rasasampanno
  gandhasampanno evameva kho mahārāja nibbānaṃ guṇavaṇṇasampannaṃ
  amatarasasampannaṃ sīlagandhasampannaṃ. Ime kho mahārāja sappimaṇḍassa
  tayo guṇā nibbāne anupaviṭṭhāti.
      Katame bhante girisikharassa pañca guṇā nibbāne anupaviṭṭhāti.
  Yathā mahārāja girisikharaṃ accuggataṃ acalaṃ anabhirūhanaṃ sabbavījānaṃ
  avirūhanaṃ anunayapaṭighavippamuttaṃ evameva kho mahārāja nibbānaṃ
  accuggataṃ acalaṃ anabhirūhanaṃ sabbakilesānaṃ avirūhanaṃ sabbakilesānaṃ
  anunayapaṭighavippamuttaṃ. Ime kho mahārāja girisikharassa pañca
-------------------------------------------------
[page 404]
  Guṇā nibbāne anupaviṭṭhāti. Sādhu bhante nāgasena evametaṃ
  tathā sampaṭicchāmīti.
                    Nibbānapañho pañcamo.
      Bhante nāgasena tumhe bhaṇatha nibbānaṃ na atītaṃ na anāgataṃ
  na paccuppannaṃ na uppannaṃ na anuppannaṃ na uppādaniyanti. Idha
  bhante nāgasena yo koci sammāpaṭipanno nibbānaṃ sacchikaroti so
  uppannaṃ sacchikaroti udāhu uppādetvā sacchikarotīti. Yo koci
  mahārāja sammāpaṭipanno nibbānaṃ sacchikaroti so na uppannaṃ
  sacchikaroti na uppādetvā sacchikaroti. Apica mahārāja atthi esā
  nibbānadhātu yaṃ so sammāpaṭipanno sacchikarotīti. Mā maṃ bhante
  nāgasena imaṃ pañhaṃ paṭacchannaṃ katvā dīpehi vivaṭaṃ pākaṭaṃ katvā
  dīpehi chandajāto ussāhajāto yaṃ te sikkhitaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ ettha
  ācikkhāhi etthāyaṃ jano sammuḷho vimatijāto saṃsayappatto bhindetaṃ
  antodosasallanti.
      Atthesā mahārāja nibbānadhātu santā sukhā paṇītā tattha
  sammāpaṭipanno jinānusitthiyā saṅkhāre sammasanto paññāya
  sacchikaroti. Yathā mahārāja antevāsiko nāma ācariyānusitthiyā
  vijjaṃ paññāya sacchikaroti evameva kho mahārāja sammāpaṭipanno
  jinānusitthiyā paññāya nibbānaṃ sacchikaroti. Kathañca pana taṃ
  nibbānaṃ daṭṭhabbanti. Anītikanirupaddavasantato abhayato khemato sukhato
  sātato paṇītato sucito sītalato daṭṭhabbaṃ. Yathā nāma mahārāja
-------------------------------------------------
[page 405]
  Puriso bahukaṭṭhapuñjena jalitakaṭhitena agginā pīḷiyamāno vāyāmena
  tato muñcitvā niraggikaṃ okāsaṃ paviseyya so tattha paramasukhaṃ
  labheyya evameva kho mahārāja yo koci sammāpaṭipanno
  tividhajalitakaṭhitāgginā pīḷiyamāno yoniso manasikārena tato muñcitvā
  byapagatatividhaggisantāpaṃ okāsaṃ paviseyya so tattha paramasukhaṃ
  nibbānaṃ sacchikaroti. Yathā mahārāja aggi evaṃ tividhaggi daṭṭhabbo
  yathā aggigato puriso evaṃ sammāpaṭipanno daṭṭhabbo yathā
  niraggikokāso evaṃ nibbānaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ. Yathā vāpana mahārāja
  puriso ahikukkuramanussakuṇapakaṇṭhakantaragato kuṇapajaṭājaṭitalaggito
  vāyāmena tato muñcitvā nikuṇapaṃ okāsaṃ paviseyya so tattha
  paramasukhaṃ labheyya evameva kho mahārāja yo sammāpaṭipanno so
  yoniso manasikārena byapagatakilesakuṇapaṃ paramasukhaṃ nibbānaṃ
  sacchikaroti. Yathā mahārāja kuṇapaṃ evaṃ pañcakāmaguṇā yathā vāyāmiko
  puriso evaṃ sammāpaṭipanno yathā nikkuṇapaokāso evaṃ nibbānaṃ.
  Yathā vā pana mahārāja puriso bhayabhīto tasito viparitakampitabhantacitto
  vāyāmena tato muñcitvā daḷhaṃ thiraṃ acalaṃ abhayaṭṭhānamupagaccheyya
  so tattha paramasukhaṃ labheyya evameva kho mahārāja yo
  sammāpaṭipanno so yoniso manasikārena byapagatabhayasantāsaṃ paramasukhaṃ
  nibbānaṃ sacchikaroti. Yathā mahārāja bhayaṃ evaṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ
  paṭicca aparāparaṃ pavattabhayaṃ yathā bhīto puriso evaṃ sammāpaṭipanno
  yathā abhayaṭṭhānaṃ evaṃ nibbānaṃ. Yathā vā pana mahārāja
-------------------------------------------------
[page 406]
  Kiliṭṭhamattikakalalakaddamagato puriso vāyāmena kalalaṃ apanetvā
  parisuddhavimaladesaṃ upagaccheyya so tattha paramasukhaṃ labheyya evameva
  kho mahārāja yo sammāpaṭipanno so yoniso manasikārena
  byapagatakilesamalakaddamaṃ paramasukhaṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikaroti. Yathā mahārāja
  kalalaṃ evaṃ lābhasakkārasiloko yathā vāyāmiko puriso evaṃ
  sammāpaṭipanno yathā parisuddhavimaladeso evaṃ nibbānaṃ.
      Tañca pana nibbānaṃ kinti sacchikaroti. Yo mahārāja
  sammāpaṭipanno so saṅkhārānaṃ pavattaṃ sammasati pavattaṃ
  sammasamāno tattha jātiṃ passati jarābyādhimaraṇaṃ na tattha kiñci sukhaṃ
  sātaṃ passati āditopi majjhatopi pariyosānatopi so tattha na
  kiñci gayhupagaṃ passati. Yathā mahārāja puriso nāma divasaṃ
  santatte ayoguḷe jalite tattakajāte āditopi majjhatopi
  pariyosānatopi na kiñci gayhupagaṃ desaṃ passati evameva kho
  mahārāja sammāpaṭipanno saṅkhārānaṃ pavattaṃ sammasati pavattaṃ
  sammasamāno tattha jātiṃ passati jarābyādhimaraṇaṃ na tattha kiñci
  sukhaṃ sātaṃ passati āditopi majjhatopi pariyosānatopi na kiñci
  gayhupagaṃ passati. Tassa gayhupagaṃ apassantassa citte arati
  saṇṭhāti kāyasmiṃ dāho okkamati so atāṇo asaraṇo asaraṇībhūto
  bhavesu nibbindati. Yathā nāma mahārāja puriso jalitajālaṃ mahantaṃ
  aggikkhandhaṃ pavisitvā jālājālaṃ anupaviṭṭho so tattha atāṇo
  asaraṇo asaraṇībhūto aggimhi nibbindati evameva kho mahārāja
-------------------------------------------------
[page 407]
  Tassa gayhupagaṃ apassantassa citte arati saṇṭhāti kāyasmiṃ
  dāho okkamati so atāṇo asaraṇo asaraṇībhūto bhavesu
  nibbindati. Tassa pavatte bhayadassāvissa evaṃ uppajjati cittaṃ
  santattaṃ kho panidaṃ pavattaṃ ādittaṃ bahudukkhaṃ bahupāyāsaṃ dissati
  bhavesu yadi koci labhetha appavattaṃ sakalaṃ etaṃ santaṃ etaṃ
  paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamatho sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo taṇhakkhayo
  virāgo nirodho nibbānanti. Itihidaṃ tassa appavatte cittaṃ
  pakkhandati pasīdati hāsayati kuhiyati paṭiladdhaṃ kho me nissaraṇanti.
  Yathā mahārāja puriso vippanaṭṭho vidisaṃ apekkhanto nibbāhanasakaṭapathaṃ
  disvā tattha pakkhandati pasīdati hāsayati kuhiyati paṭiladdhaṃ
  me nibbāhanasakaṭapathanti evameva kho mahārāja tassa pavatte
  bhayadassāvissa appavatte cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati hāsayati kuhiyati
  paṭiladdhaṃ kho me nissaraṇanti. So appavattāya maggaṃ āyūhati
  āsevati bhāveti bahulīkaroti tassa tadatthāya sati saṇṭhāti tadatthāya
  viriyaṃ santiṭṭhati tadatthāya pīti santiṭṭhati tassa taṃ cittaṃ aparāparaṃ
  manasikaroto pavattaṃ samatikkamitvā appavattaṃ okkamati. Appavattaṃ
  anuppatto mahārāja sammāpaṭipanno nibbānaṃ sacchikarotīti vuccatīti.
  Sādhu bhante nāgasena evametaṃ tathā sampaṭicchāmīti.
                 Nibbānasacchikaraṇapañho chaṭṭho.
      Bhante nāgasena atthi so padeso puratthimāya vā disāya
  dakkhiṇāya vā disāya pacchimāya vā disāya uttarāya vā disāya
-------------------------------------------------
[page 408]
  Uddhaṃ vā adho vā tiriyaṃ vā yattha nibbānaṃ sannihitaṃ hotīti.
  Natthi so mahārāja padeso yattha taṃ nibbānaṃ sannihitaṃ hotīti.
  Yadi bhante nāgasena natthi nibbānassa sannihitokāso tenahi
  nibbānaṃ natthi yesañca taṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikataṃ tesampi sacchikiriyā
  micchā. Tattha kāraṇaṃ vakkhāmi. Yathā bhante nāgasena mahiyā
  dhaññuṭṭhānaṃ khettaṃ atthi gandhuṭṭhānaṃ pupphaṃ atthi pupphuṭṭhānaṃ
  gumbo atthi phaluṭṭhānaṃ rukkho atthi ratanuṭṭhānaṃ ākaro atthi
  tattha yo koci yaṃ yaṃ icchati so tattha gantvā taṃ taṃ āharati
  evameva kho bhante nāgasena yadi nibbānaṃ atthi tassa nibbānassa
  upādānokāso icchitabbo. Yasmā ca bhante nāgasena
  nibbānassa upādānokāso natthi tasmā kāraṇāhaṃ brūmi natthi
  nibbānanti yesañca taṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikataṃ tesampi sacchikiriyā
  micchāti. Natthi mahārāja nibbānassa nikkhittokāso apica
  nibbānaṃ atthi sammāpaṭipanno yoniso manasikārena saṃkhārānaṃ
  udayabbayaṃ sammasamāno nibbānaṃ sacchikaroti. Yathā mahārāja
  aggi nāma atthi natthi tassa nikkhittokāso dve kaṭṭhāni
  saṃghaṭṭento aggiṃ adhigacchati evameva kho mahārāja nibbānaṃ atthi
  nibbānassa ca nikkhittokāso natthi sammāpaṭipanno yoniso
  manasikārena saṃkhārānaṃ udayabbayaṃ sammasamāno nibbānaṃ sacchikaroti.
  Yathā vā pana mahārāja satta ratanāni nāma atthi seyyathīdaṃ
  cakkaratanaṃ hatthiratanaṃ assaratanaṃ maṇiratanaṃ itthīratanaṃ gahapatiratanaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 409]
  Parināyakaratananti na ca tesaṃ ratanānaṃ nikkhittokāso atthi
  khattiyānañca sammāpaṭipannānaṃ paṭipattiphalena tāni satta ratanāni upagacchanti
  evameva kho mahārāja nibbānaṃ atthi nibbānassa ca nikkhittokāso
  natthi sammāpaṭipanno yoniso manasikārena saṃkhārānaṃ udayabbayaṃ
  sammasamāno nibbānaṃ sacchikarotīti.
      Bhante nāgasena yadi nibbānassa nikkhittokāso natthi atthi
  pana taṃ ṭhānaṃ yattha ṭhito sammāpaṭipanno nibbānaṃ sacchikarotīti.
  Āma mahārāja atthi taṃ ṭhānaṃ yattha ṭhito sammāpaṭipanno
  nibbānaṃ sacchikarotīti. Katamaṃ bhante nāgasena taṃ ṭhānaṃ yattha
  ṭhito sammāpaṭipanno nibbānaṃ sacchikaroti taṃ ṭhānaṃpi mayaṃ labhāma
  savanāyāti. Sīlaṃ mahārāja ṭhānaṃ sīle patiṭṭhito yoniso
  manasikaronto sammāpaṭipanno sakayavanepi cīnavilātepi alasandepi
  nikumbepi kāsikosalepi kasmirepi gandhārepi nagamuddhanipi
  barahmalokepi yattha katthacipi ṭhito sammāpaṭipanno nibbānaṃ sacchikaroti.
  Yathā mahārāja yo koci cakkhumā puriso sakayavanepi cīnavilātepi
  alasandepi nikumbepi kāsikosalepi kasmirepi gandhārepi nagamuddhanipi
  brahamalokepi yattha katthacipi ṭhito ākāsaṃ passati evameva kho
  mahārāja sīle patiṭṭhito yoniso manasikaronto sakayavanepi .pe.
  Yattha katthacipi ṭhito sammāpaṭipanno nibbānaṃ sacchikaroti.
  Yathā pana mahārāja sakayavanepi .pe. Yattha katthacipi ṭhitassa
-------------------------------------------------
[page 410]
  Pubbadisā atthi evameva kho mahārāja sīle patiṭṭhitassa yoniso
  manasikarontassa sakayavanepi .pe. Yattha katthacipi ṭhitassa
  sammāpaṭipannassa nibbānassa sacchikiriyā atthīti. Sādhu bhante
  nāgasena desitaṃ tayā nibbānaṃ desitā nibbānassa sacchikiriyā
  ussāpito dhammadhajo saṇṭhāpitā dhammanetti evametaṃ tathā
  sampaṭicchāmīti.
                Nibbānassapaṭṭhānapañho sattamo.
      Atha kho so milindo rājā kavinā saddhiṃ sārāṇiyaṃ anupaviṭṭho
  ñātukāmo sotukāmo dhāretukāmo ñāṇālokaṃ daṭṭhukāmo añāṇaṃ
  bhinditukāmo ñāṇaṃ uppādetukāmo avijjaṃ nāsetukāmo
  saṃsārasotaṃ samatikkamitukāmo taṇhāsotaṃ chanditukāmo amataṃ
  pātukāmo nibbānaṃ phussitukāmo adhimattaṃ chandañca adhimattaṃ
  vāyāmañca dhitiñca ussāhañca satisampajaññañca upathambhetvā theraṃ
  nāgasenaṃ evaṃ samapucchi
      kiṃ pana bhante nāgasena buddho tayā diṭṭhoti. Thero
  āha na hi mahārājāti. Kiṃ pana te ācariyehi buddho
  diṭṭhoti. Na hi mahārājāti. Bhante nāgasena na kira tayā
  buddho diṭṭho nāpi kira te ācāriyehi buddho diṭṭho. Tenahi
  bhante nāgasena natthi buddho na hettha paññāyatīti. Thero
  āha atthi pana te mahārāja pubbakā khattiyā ye tava
  khattiyavaṃsassa pubbaṅgamāti. Āma bhante ko saṃsayo atthi pubbakā
-------------------------------------------------
[page 411]
  Khattiyā ye ca mama khattiyavaṃsassa pubbaṅgamāti. Diṭṭhapubbā pana
  tayā mahārāja pubbakā khattiyāti. Na hi bhanteti. Ye pana
  taṃ mahārāja anusāsanti purohitā senāpatino akkhadassā mahāmattā
  dhuttisaddhipāsāmanikā satthavāhā etehi pubbakā khattiyā
  diṭṭhapubbāti. Na hi bhanteti. Yadi khalu na te mahārāja pubbakā
  khattiyā diṭṭhā nāpi kira te anusāsakehi pubbakā khattiyā diṭṭhā
  kattha pubbākā khattiyā na hettha pubbakā khattiyā paññāyantīti.
  Dissanti bhante nāgasena pubbakānaṃ khattiyānaṃ anubhūtā paribhogiyā
  bhaṇḍā seyyathīdaṃ setacchattaṃ uṇhīsaṃ bāḷavījanī mahārahāni
  sayanāni khaggaratanañcāti yehi mayaṃ jāneyyāma saddaheyyāma atthi
  te pubbakā khattiyā ye mama khattiyavaṃsassa pubbaṅgamāti.
  Evameva kho mahārāja mayaṃpetaṃ bhagavantaṃ jāneyyāma saddaheyyāma
  atthi taṃ kāraṇaṃ yena kāraṇena jānāma saddahāma atthi so
  bhagavāti. Katamaṃ taṃ kāraṇaṃ. Atthi kho mahārāja tena bhagavatā
  jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhena anubhūtā paribhogiyā
  bhaṇḍā seyyathīdaṃ cattāro satipaṭṭhānā cattāro sammappadhānā
  cattāro iddhipādā pañcindriyāni pañca balāni satta bojjhaṅgā
  ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggoti yena yena sadevako loko jānāti
  saddahati atthi so bhagavāti. Iminā ca mahārāja kāraṇena
  iminā hetunā iminā nayena iminā anumānena ñātabbaṃ atthi
  so bhagavāti.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 412]
         Bahū jane tārayitvā nibbuto upadhikkhaye
         anumānena ñātabbo atthi so dipaduttamoti.
      Bhante nāgasena upamaṃ karohīti. Yathā mahārāja
  nagaravaḍḍhakī nagaraṃ māpetukāmo paṭhamantāva bhūmiṃ anuviloketvā samaṃ
  anunnataṃ anonataṃ asakkharapāsāṇañca nirupaddavaṃ anavajjaṃ rammaṇīyaṃ
  bhūmibhāgaṃ sabbantaṃ passitvā yaṃ tattha visamaṃ taṃ samaṃ kārāpetvā
  khāṇukaṇṭakaṃ sodhāpetvā nagaraṃ sobhanaṃ tattha māpeyya vibhajjitaṃ
  bhāgaso okiṇṇaparikkhapākāraṃ daḷhāṭṭālakoṭṭhakaṃ
  puthucaccaracatukkoṇasandhisiṃghāṭakaṃ sucisamatalarājamaggaṃ suvibhattāntarāpaṇaṃ
  ārāmuyyānataḷākapokkharaṇīudakapānasampannaṃ sabbadosavirahitaṃ so tasmiṃ
  nagare sabbathā vepullaṃ patte aññaṃ desaṃ upagaccheyya. Atha taṃ
  nagaraṃ aparena samayena iddhaṃ bhaveyya phītaṃ subhikkhaṃ sukhemaṃ susamiddhaṃ
  sivaṃ anītikaṃ nirupaddavaṃ nānājanasamākulaṃ puthū khattiyā brāhmaṇā
  vessā suddā hatthārohā assārohā rathikā pattikā dhanuggahā
  ceṭakā piṇḍakāpikā uggā rājaputtā pakkhandino mahānāgā
  sūrā vammino yodhino dāsaputtā bhattiputtā mallagaṇā ālārikā
  kappakā nhāpakā cundā mālākārā suvaṇṇakārā sajjhukārā
  sīsakārā tipukārā lohakārā ayakārā maṇikārā daṇḍakārā
  ghaṭakārā kavacakārā kuṇḍalakārā kammañjatukārā aṅgadakārā
  māsakārā pesakārā kumbhakārā gandibhedā balakārā bhedanakārā
  kammakārā rajjukārā dantakārā kocchakārā suttakārā tiṇakārā
-------------------------------------------------
[page 413]
  Vālukārā mattikakārā asikārā sattikārā kuntikārā kodaṇḍakārā
  kambalakārā gandhakārā khettakārā vatthukārā gāmakārā nigamakārā
  nagarakārā dhitikārā gharakārā setakārā nīlakārā pītakārā lohitakārā
  raṅgakārā vaṇṇakārā chavikārā cittakārā sotthakārā kaṭṭhakārā
  rūpakārā rattakārā piyakārā guṇakārā setukārā veṭṭhakārā
  sāgarapokkharaṇīkārā suvaṇṇakārakoṇovikārā gaṇṭhikārā tantavāyā
  tunnavāyā hiraññakārā dussakārā rajakā rajakārā molikārā
  avakārā kūpakārā cammakārā tacakārā rathakārā vāpassikārā
  pulikārā pakkamaṃsakārā bhasarūpiphalikā vaṇṇikā puvikā macchikā
  gaṇikā kumbhadāsī kutalā damisā malavālakāsikaṇḍaparavāhivā bāhikā
  nigahā rūpavāhihikanagaharasaṃvaccharā akaṇṇakā sakaṇṇakā sakayavanacīnavilātā
  ujjenakā bhārukacchakā sāketakā kosambhakā utumbakamadhukā
  parantakā okālakā pekhaṇasākakalakā koseyyakā liṅgavā
  dalhakā javadīpakā takosāverapathā ceva aṅgalasaṇṭhā kismiragaṇṭhā
  visayinopi taṃ nagaraṃ vāsāya upagatā nānāvisayino janā nagaraṃ
  suvibhattaṃ adosaṃ anavajjaṃ passitvā anumānena jānanti cheko
  vata bho so vaḍḍhakī bhavissati yo imassa māpetāti evameva
  kho mahārāja so bhagavā asamo asamasamo appaṭisamo aggo
  asadiso atuliyo asaṃkheyyo appameyyo amitaguṇo guṇapāramipatto
  anantadhiti anantatejo anantabalo anantaviriyo buddhabalapāramiṃgato
  sasenaṃ māraṃ bhinditvā diṭaṭhijālaṃ padālayitvā avijjaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 414]
  Vidhaṃsayitvā vijjaṃ uppādetvā dhammacakkaṃ dhārayitvā sabbaññutaṃ
  pāpuṇitvā nijjitavijitasaṅgāmo dhammanagaraṃ māpesi.
      Bhagavato kho mahārāja dhammanagaraṃ sīlapākāraṃ hiriparikkhaṃ
  satidovārikaṃ ñāṇadvārakoṭṭhakaṃ viriyāddālakaṃ saddhāesikaṃ
  paññāpāsādaṃ suttantacaccaraṃ abhidhammasiṃghāṭakaṃ vinayavinicchayaṃ
  satipaṭṭhānavīthikaṃ. Tassa kho mahārāja satipaṭṭhānavīthiyā evarūpā āpaṇā
  pasāritā honti seyyathīdaṃ pupphāpaṇaṃ gandhāpaṇaṃ phalāpaṇaṃ agadāpaṇaṃ
  osathāpaṇaṃ amatāpaṇaṃ ratanāpaṇaṃ sabbāpaṇanti.
      Bhante nāgasena katamaṃ buddhassa bhagavato pupphāpaṇanti.
  Atthi kho mahārāja tena bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā
  sammāsambuddhena ārammaṇavibhattiyo ācikkhitā seyyathīdaṃ aniccasaññā
  anattasaññā asubhasaññā ādīnavasaññā pahānasaññā virāgasaññā
  nirodhasaññā sabbaloke anabhiratasaññā sabbasaṅkhāresu anicchasaññā
  aṭṭhikasaññā pulavakasaññā vinīlakasaññā vicchiddakasaññā
  vipubbakasaññā uddhumātakasaññā vikkhāyitakasaññā hatavikkhittakasaññā
  lohitakasaññā mettasaññā karuṇāsaññā muditāsaññā upekkhāsaññā
  ānāpānasati maraṇasati kāyagatāsati. Imā kho mahārāja
  buddhena bhagavatā ārammaṇavibhattiyo ācikkhitā. Tattha yo koci
  jarāmaraṇā muccitukāmo so tesu aññataraṃ ārammaṇaṃ gaṇhāti
  tena ārammaṇena rāgā vimuccati dosā vimuccati mohā vimuccati
  mānato vimuccati diṭṭhito vimuccati saṃsāraṃ tarati taṇhāsotaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 415]
  Nivāreti vimatiṃ chindati sabbakilese upahantvā amalaṃ virajaṃ suddhaṃ
  paṇḍaraṃ ajātaṃ ajaraṃ amaraṃ sukhaṃ sītibhūtaṃ abhayaṃ nagaruttamaṃ
  nibbānanagaraṃ pavisitvā arahatte cittaṃ vimoceti. Idaṃ vuccati
  mahārāja bhagavato pupphāpaṇanti.
         Kammamūlaṃ gahetvāna upagacchanti āpaṇaṃ
         ārammaṇaṃ kiṇitvāna pupphanti te vimuttiyāti.
  Kallosi bhante nāgasenāti.
      Bhante nāgasena katamaṃ buddhassa bhagavato gandhāpaṇanti.
  Atthi kho mahārāja tena bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā
  sammāsambuddhena sīlavibhattiyo ācikkhitā yena sīlagandhena anulittā
  bhagavato puttā sadevakaṃ lokaṃ sīlagandhena dhūpenti sampadhūpenti
  disaṃpi vidisaṃpi anuvātaṃpi paṭivātaṃpi vāyanti ativāyanti pharitvā
  tiṭṭhanti. Katamā pana tā sīlavibhattiyo. Tīṇi ca saraṇagamanāni
  pañcasīlāni aṭṭhaṅgasīladasaṅgasīlāni pañcuddesapariyāpannāni ca sīlāni
  pāṭimokkhapariyāpannāni sīlāni. Idaṃ vuccati mahārāja bhagavato
  gandhāpaṇanti. Bhāsitaṃ hetaṃ mahārāja bhagavatā devātidevena
          na pupphagandho paṭivātameti
          na candanaṃ tagaramallikā vā
          satañca gandho paṭivātameti
          sabbā disā sappuriso pavāyati.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 416]
          Candanaṃ tagaraṃ vāpi uppalaṃ atha vassikī
          etesaṃ gandhajātānaṃ sīlagandho anuttaro
          appamatto ayaṃ gandho yvāyaṃ tagaracandanī
          yo ca sīlavataṃ gandho vāti devesu uttamoti.
  Kallosi bhante nāgasenāti.
      Bhante nāgasena katamaṃ buddhassa bhagavato phalāpaṇanti.
  Phalāni kho mahārāja bhagavatā akkhātāni seyyathīdaṃ sotāpattiphalaṃ
  sakadāgāmiphalaṃ anāgāmiphalaṃ arahattaphalaṃ suññataphalasamāpatti
  animittaphalasamāpatti appaṇihitaphalasamāpatti aneñjaphalasamāpatti
  upekkhāphalasamāpatti. Tattha yo koci yaṃ phalaṃ icchati so kammamūlaṃ
  gahetvā patthitaṃ phalaṃ kīṇāti yadi sotāpattiphalaṃ .pe. Yadi
  upekkhāphalasamāpattiṃ. Yathā mahārāja ekassa janassa dhuvaphalo
  ambo bhaveyya na so tāva tato phalaṃ nipāteti na yāva kayikā
  āgacchanti. Anuppatte pana kayike mūlaṃ gahetvā evaṃ ācikkhati
  ambho purisa eso kho ambo dhuvaphalo tato yaṃ icchasi
  ettakaṃ nāma phalaṃ gaṇhāhi salāṭukaṃ vā dovilaṃ vā kesikaṃ
  vā āmaṃ vā bheṅgavaṇṇaṃ vā pakkavaṇṇaṃ vāti. So tena
  attanā dinnamūlena yadi salāṭukaṃ icchati salāṭukaṃ gaṇhāti yadi
  dovilaṃ icchati dovilaṃ gaṇhāti yadi kesikaṃ icchati kesikaṃ
  gaṇhāti yadi āmaṃ icchati āmaṃ gaṇhāti yadi bheṅgavaṇṇaṃ
  icchati bheṅgavaṇṇaṃ gaṇhāti yadi pakkavaṇṇaṃ icchati pakkavaṇṇaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 417]
  Gaṇhāti. Evameva kho mahārāja yo koci yaṃ yaṃ phalaṃ icchati
  so kammamūlaṃ datvā patthitaṃ phalaṃ gaṇhāti yadi sotāpattiphalaṃ
  .pe. Yadi upekkhāphalasamāpattinti. Tatretaṃ vuccati buddhassa
  bhagavato phalāpaṇanti.
         Kammamūlañca datvāna gaṇhanti amatapphalaṃ
         tena te sukhitā loke ye kītā amatapphalanti.
  Kallosi bhante nāgasenāti.
      Bhante nāgasena katamaṃ buddhassa bhagavato agadāpaṇanti.
  Agadāni kho mahārāja bhagavatā akkhātāni yehi agadehi so bhagavā
  sadevamanusse satte kilesavisato parimocesi. Katamāni pana tāni
  agadāni. Yānimāni mahārāja bhagavatā cattāri ariyasaccāni
  akkhātāni seyyathīdaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasaccaṃ dukkhasamudayo ariyasaccaṃ
  dukkhanirodho ariyasaccaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasaccaṃ.
  Tattha ye keci aññāpekkhā catusaccaṃ dhammaṃ suṇanti.
  Jātidhammā sattā jātiyā parimuccanti. Jarādhammā sattā jarāya
  parimuccanti. Maraṇadhammā sattā maraṇā parimuccanti.
  Sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsadhammā sattā tehi sabbehi parimucacanti.
  Tatretaṃ vuccati buddhassa bhagavato agadāpaṇanti.
         Ye keci loke agadā visānaṃ paṭibāhakā
         dhammāgadasamaṃ natthi etaṃ pivatha bhikkhavoti.
  Kallosi bhante nāgasenāti.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 418]
      Bhante nāgasena katamaṃ buddhassa bhagavato osathāpaṇanti.
  Osathāni kho mahārāja bhagavatā akkhātāni yehi osathehi so
  bhagavā sadevamanusse satte tikicchati. Katamāni pana tāni osathāni.
  Cattāro satipaṭṭhānā cattāro sammappadhānā cattāro iddhipādā
  pañcindriyāni pañca balāni satta sambojjhaṅgā ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko
  maggo. Etehi osathehi bhagavā micchādiṭṭhiṃ vireceti micchāsaṅkappaṃ
  vireceti micchāvācaṃ vireceti micchākammantaṃ vireceti micchāājīvaṃ
  vireceti micchāvāyāmaṃ vireceti micchāsatiṃ vireceti micchāsamādhiṃ
  vireceti. Imāni kho mahārāja bhagavato osathāni yehi osathehi
  so bhagavā sadevamanusse satte sabbabhayā parimoceti sabbapāsā
  parimoceti sabbadukkhā parimoceti sabbakantārā parimoceti pamoceti
  nimoceti ārādheti cittaṃ. Tatretaṃ vuccati buddhassa bhagavato
  osathāpaṇanti.
         Ye keci osathā loke saṃvijjanti vividhā bahū
         dhammosathasamaṃ natthi etaṃ pivatha bhikkhavoti.
  Supasāritaṃ bhante nāgasena bhagavato osathāpaṇaṃ. Atthi pana
  koci satto bhagavato osathaṃ pivitvā yo sukhī ahosīti.
  Sutapubbaṃ pana tayā mahārāja sāmāyike nimitte pucchanti
  bhagavā visajjesi bhagavā pucchite nimitte visajjetīti. Sutapubbaṃ
  mayā bhanteti. Ete caññe ca devamanussā mahārāja bhagavatā
  visajjitā.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 419]
         Dhammosathaṃ pivitvāna ajarāmaraṇā siyuṃ
         tāva nibbānaṃ ca phussitvā nibbutā upadhikkhayeti.
  Kallosi bhante nāgasenāti.
      Bhante nāgasena katamaṃ buddhassa bhagavato amatāpaṇanti. Amataṃ
  kho mahārāja bhagavatā akkhātaṃ yena amatena so bhagavā
  sadevamanusse satte abhisiñci. Yena amatena abhisiñcitā devamanussā
  jātijarābyādhimaraṇasokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsehi parimucciṃsu.
  Katamaṃ taṃ amataṃ. Yadidaṃ kāyagatā sati. Bhāsitametaṃ mahārāja
  bhagavatā devātidevena amatante bhikkhave paribhuñjanti ye kāyagatāsatiṃ
  paribhuñjantīti. Tatretaṃ vuccati buddhassa bhagavato amatāpaṇanti.
         Byādhitaṃ janataṃ disvā amatāpaṇaṃ pasārayi
         kammena taṃ kiṇitvāna amataṃ ādetha bhikkhavoti.
  Kallosi bhante nāgasenāti.
      Bhante nāgasena katamaṃ buddhassa bhagavato ratanāpaṇanti.
  Ratanāni kho mahārāja bhagavatā akkhātāni yehi ratanehi pilandhā
  bhagavato puttā sadevakaṃ lokaṃ virocanti obhāsenti pabhāsenti
  jalanti pajjalanti uddhaṃ adho tiriyaṃ ālokaṃ dassenti. Katamāni
  pana tāni ratanāni. Sīlaratanaṃ samādhiratanaṃ paññāratanaṃ vimuttiratanaṃ
  vimuttiñāṇadassanaratanaṃ paṭisambhidāratanaṃ bojjhaṅgaratanaṃ. Katamaṃ
  mahārāja bhagavato sīlaratanaṃ. Pāṭimokkhasaṃvarasīlaṃ indriyasaṃvarasīlaṃ
  ājīvapārisuddhisīlaṃ paccayasannissitasīlaṃ cūḷasīlaṃ majjhimasīlaṃ mahāsīlaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 420]
  Maggasīlaṃ phalasīlaṃ. Sīlaratanena kho mahārāja pilandhassa puggalassa
  sadevako loko samārako sabrahmako sassamaṇabrāhmaṇī pajā
  sadevamanussā pihayati pattheti. Sīlaratanapilandho kho mahārāja
  bhikkhu disaṃpi atirocati vidisaṃpi atirocati disāvidisaṃpi atirocati
  uddhaṃpi atirocati adhopi atirocati uddhaṃadhopi atirocati
  omasitvā avīciṃ adho omasitvā bhavaggaṃ uddhaṃ etthantare sabbaratanāni
  atikkamitvā saṃkhipitvā atighāpitvā abhibhavitvā ajjhottharitvā
  tiṭṭhati. Evarūpāni kho mahārāja sīlaratanāni bhagavato ratanāpaṇe
  pasāriyanti. Tatretaṃ vuccati buddhassa bhagavato sīlaratananti.
  Bhāsitaṃ hetaṃ mahārāja bhagavatā devātidevena
        sīlaseyyo sutaseyyo iti me saṃsayā ahu
        sīlameva sutā seyyo iti me natthi saṃsayoti.
        Evarūpāni sīlāni vijjanti mama āpaṇe
        kammena taṃ kiṇitvāna ratanaṃ tumhe pilandhathāti.
      Katamaṃ pana taṃ mahārāja bhagavato samādhiratanaṃ.
  Savitakkasavicārasamādhi avitakkasavicārasamādhi avitakkāvicārasamādhi suññato
  samādhi animitto samādhi appaṇihito samādhi. Samādhiratanaṃ kho
  mahārāja pilandhassa bhikkhuno ye te kāmabyāpādavihiṃsāvitakkā
  mānuddhaccadiṭṭhivikicchākilesavatthūni vividhāni ca kuvitakkāni te sabbe
  samādhiṃ āsajja vikiranti vidhamanti vidhaṃsanti na saṇṭhahanti na
  upalimpanti. Yathā mahārāja vāri pokkharapatte vikirati vidhamati
-------------------------------------------------
[page 421]
  Vidhaṃsati na saṇṭhāti na upalimpati taṃ kissa hetu parisuddhattā
  padumassa evameva kho mahārāja samādhiratanaṃ pilandhassa bhikkhuno
  ye kāmabyāpādavihiṃsāvitakkā mānauddhaccadiṭṭhivicikicchākilesavatthūni
  vividhāni ca kuvitakkāni sabbe te samādhiṃ āsajja vikiranti vidhamanti
  vidhaṃsanti na saṇṭhahanti na upalimpanta taṃ kissa hetu parisuddhattā
  samādhissa avikkhittattā ca. Samādhiratanaṃ pilandhanto kho mahārāja
  bhikkhu sabbaṃ tamaṃ hanati abhitappeti suriyatejaṃ abhitāpeti dosinaṃ
  dhūpeti vidhūpeti. Evarūpāni kho mahārāja samādhiratanāni bhagavato
  ratanāpaṇe pasāriyanti. Tatretaṃ vuccati buddhassa bhagavato
  samādhiratananti.
         Samādhiratanamālassa kuvitakkā na jāyare
         na ca vikkhipate cittaṃ etaṃ tumhe pilandhathāti.
      Katamaṃ pana mahārāja bhagavato paññāratanaṃ. Yāya mahārāja
  paññāya ariyasāvako idaṃ kusalanti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti idaṃ akusalanti
  yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti idaṃ sāvajjaṃ idaṃ anavajjaṃ idaṃ sevitabbaṃ idaṃ na
  sevitabbaṃ idaṃ hīnaṃ idaṃ paṇītaṃ idaṃ kaṇhaṃ idaṃ sukkaṃ idaṃ kaṇhasukkaṃ
  idaṃ sappaṭibhāganti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti idaṃ dukkhanti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti
  ayaṃ dukkhasamudayoti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti ayaṃ dukkhanirodhoti yathābhūtaṃ
  pajānāti ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadāti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti.
  Evarūpāni kho mahārāja paññāratanāni bhagavato ratanāpaṇe
  pasāriyanti. Tatretaṃ vuccati buddhassa bhagavato paññāratananti.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 422]
         Paññāratanamālassa na ciraṃ vattate bhavo
         khippaṃ phusseti amataṃ na ca so rocate bhaveti.
      Katamaṃ pana mahārāja bhagavato vimuttiratanaṃ. Vimuttiratananti kho
  mahārāja arahattaṃ vuccati. Arahattaṃ patto kho mahārāja bhikkhu
  vimuttiratanaṃ pilandhoti vuccati. Yathā mahārāja puriso
  muttākalāpamaṇikanakapabāḷābharaṇapaṭimaṇḍito aggalutagaratālīsakarattacandanānulitto
  nāgasattalāpunnāgacampakādhimuttikadhanurikāuppalapadumanaḷita-
  sattapattasogandhikavassikasumanamallikāvicitto sesajane atikkamitvā
  virocati atirocati obhāsati pabhāsati sampabhāsati jalati pajjalati
  abhibhavati ajjhottharati mālāgandharatanābharaṇehi evameva kho
  mahārāja arahattaṃ patto khīṇāsavo vimuttiratanapilandho upādāyupādāya
  vimuttānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ atikkamitvā samatikkamitvā virocati atirocati
  obhāsati pabhāsati sampabhāsati jalati pajjalati abhibhavati ajjhottharati
  vimuttiyā taṃ kissa hetu aggaṃ mahārāja etaṃ pilandhanaṃ sabbapilandhanānaṃ
  yadidaṃ vimuttipilandhananti. Evarūpaṃ kho mahārāja vimuttiratanaṃ bhagavato
  ratanāpaṇe pasāriyati. Tatretaṃ vuccati buddhassa bhagavato vimuttiratananti.
         Maṇimayaṃ pilandhāya gharasāmī udikkhati
         vimuttiratanamālassa udikkhanti sadevakāti.
      Katamaṃ pana mahārāja bhagavato vimuttiñāṇadassanaratanaṃ.
  Paccavekkhañāṇaṃ mahārāja bhagavato vimuttiñāṇadassanaratananti vuccati yena
-------------------------------------------------
[page 423]
  Ñāṇena ariyasāvako maggaphalanibbānāni pahīnakilesāvasiṭṭhakilese
  ca paccavekkhati.
         Yena ñāṇena bujjhanti ariyā katakiccataṃ
         taṃ ñāṇaratanaṃ laddhuṃ vāyametha jinorasāti.
      Katamaṃ pana mahārāja bhagavato paṭisambhidāratanaṃ. Catasso
  kho mahārāja paṭisambhidāyo atthapaṭisambhidā dhammapaṭisambhidā
  niruttipaṭisambhidā paṭibhāṇapaṭisambhidāti. Imehi kho mahārāja
  catūhi paṭisambhidāratanehi pilandho bhikkhu abhīto yadi khattiyaparisaṃ
  yadi brāhmaṇaparisaṃ yadi gahapatiparisaṃ yadi samaṇaparisaṃ visārado
  amaṅkubhūto upasaṅkamati asambhīto asaṃviggo parisaṃ upasaṅkami
  anutrāso parisaṃ upasaṅkamati acchambhito parisaṃ upasaṅkamati akampito
  parisaṃ upasaṅkamati apaṭṭhāno parisaṃ upasaṅkamati aparibhāsanto
  parisaṃ upasaṅkamati vigatalomahaṃso parisaṃ upasaṅkamati anonato parisaṃ
  upasaṅkamati visaṭṭho parisaṃ upasaṅkamati sumano parisaṃ upasaṅkamati
  avimano parisaṃ upasaṅkamati hāsayanto parisaṃ upasaṅkamati tosayanto
  parisaṃ upasaṅkamati vedayanto parisaṃ upasaṅkamati nandayanto parisaṃ
  upasaṅkamati modayanto parisaṃ upasaṅkamati āmodayanto parisaṃ
  upasaṅkamati pamodayanto parisaṃ upasaṅkamati saññāpento parisaṃ
  upasaṅkamati jotayanto parisaṃ upasaṅkamati ujjotayanto parisaṃ
  upasaṅkamati obhāsayanto parisaṃ upasaṅkamati pabhāsayanto parisaṃ
  upasaṅkamati ghaṃsento parisaṃ upasaṅkamati atighaṃsento parisaṃ upasaṅkamati
-------------------------------------------------
[page 424]
  Tappento parisaṃ upasaṅkamati atikkamento parisaṃ upasaṅkamati
  uttharanto parisaṃ upasaṅkamati samottharanto parisaṃ upasaṅkamati
  nādayanto parisaṃ upasaṅkamati jitāvī parisaṃ upasaṅkamati vijitāvī
  parisaṃ upasaṅkamati asamasamo parisaṃ upasaṅkamati appaṭisamo parisaṃ
  upasaṅkamati appaṭibhāgo parisaṃ upasaṅkamati sabbesaṃ patthito parisaṃ
  upasaṅkamati. Yathā mahārāja puriso saṅgāmasūro akkhittayuddhakalāpo
  cittadhanuṃ sattiṃ maṇḍalaggaṃ kaṇayaṃ kuntimantaṃ ca pañcāvudhe
  sannayha asambhītova saṅgāmaṃ otarati sace amittā dure
  bhavissanti usunā pātayissāmi tato orato bhavissanti sattiyā
  pātayissāmi tato orato bhavissanti kaṇayena paharissāmi upagataṃ
  santaṃ maṇḍalaggena dvidhā chindissāmi kāyūpagataṃ kuntimantena
  vinivijjhissāmīti evaṃ so mahārāja puriso saṅgāmasūro cintayitvā
  asambhīto saṅgāmaṃ otarati evameva kho mahārāja catūhi
  paṭisambhidāratanehi pilandho bhikkhu abhīto parisaṃ upasaṅkamati .pe.
  Sabbesaṃ janānaṃ patthito upasaṅkamati yo koci maṃ atthapaṭisambhidāya
  pañhaṃ pucchissati tassa atthena atthaṃ kathayissāmi kāraṇena
  kāraṇaṃ kathayissāmi hetunā hetuṃ kathayissāmi nayena nayaṃ kathayissāmi
  nissaṃsayaṃ karissāmi vimatiṃ vivecessāmi tosayissāmi
  pañhābyākaraṇena. Yo koci maṃ dhammapaṭisambhidāya pañhaṃ pucchissati
  tassa dhammena dhammaṃ kathayissāmi amatena amataṃ kathayissāmi asaṃkhatena
  asaṃkhataṃ kathayissāmi nibbānena nibbānaṃ kathayissāmi suññatāya
-------------------------------------------------
[page 425]
  Suññataṃ kathayissāmi animittena animittaṃ kathayissāmi appaṇihitena
  appaṇihitaṃ kathayissāmi aneñjena aneñjaṃ kathayissāmi upekkhāya
  upekkhaṃ kathayissāmi nissaṃsayaṃ karissāmi vimatiṃ vivecessāmi
  tosayissāmi pañhābyākaraṇena. Yo koci maṃ niruttipaṭisambhidāya
  pañhaṃ pucchissati tassa niruttiyā niruttiṃ kathayissāmi padena padaṃ
  kathayissāmi anupadena anupadaṃ kathayissāmi akkharena akkharaṃ kathayissāmi
  sandhiyā sandhiṃ kathayissāmi byañjanena byañjanaṃ kathayissāmi
  anubyañjanena anubyañjanaṃ kathayissāmi vaṇṇena vaṇṇaṃ kathayissāmi
  sarena saraṃ kathayissāmi paṇṇattiyā paṇṇattiṃ kathayissāmi vohārena
  vohāraṃ kathayissāmi nissaṃsayaṃ karissāmi vimatiṃ vivecessāmi
  tosayissāmi pañhābyākaraṇena. Yo koci paṭibhāṇapaṭisambhidāya
  pañhaṃ pucchissati tassa paṭibhāṇena paṭibhāṇaṃ kathayissāmi upamena
  upamaṃ kathayissāmi rasena rasaṃ kathayissāmi lakkhaṇena lakkhaṇaṃ
  kathayissāmi nissaṃsayaṃ karissāmi vimatiṃ vivecessāmi tosayissāmi
  pañhābyākaraṇenāti. Evameva kho mahārāja paṭisambhidāratanāni
  bhagavato ratanāpaṇe pasāriyanti. Tatretaṃ vuccati buddhassa
  bhagavato paṭisambhidāratananti.
         Paṭisambhidā kiṇitvāna ñāṇena phussayeyya so
         acchambhito anubbiggo atiroce sadevaketi.
      Katamaṃ pana mahārāja bhagavato bojjhaṅgaratanaṃ. Sattimāni
-------------------------------------------------
[page 426]
  Mahārāja bojjhaṅgaratanāni yehi pilandho bhikkhu sabbaṃ tamaṃ abhibhuyya
  sadevakaṃ lokaṃ obhāseti pabhāseti ālokaṃ janeti. Katamāni satta.
  Satisambojjhaṅgaratanaṃ dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgaratanaṃ viriyasambojjhaṅgaratanaṃ
  pītisambojjhaṅgaratanaṃ passaddhisambojjhaṅgaratanaṃ samādhisambojjhaṅgaratanaṃ
  upekkhāsambojjhaṅgaratanaṃ. Imāni kho mahārāja satta
  sambojjhaṅgaratanāni yehi pilandho bhikkhu sabbaṃ tamaṃ abhibhuyya
  sadevakaṃ lokaṃ obhāseti pabhāseti ālokaṃ janeti. Tatretaṃ
  vuccati buddhassa bhagavato bojjhaṅgaratananti.
         Bojjhaṅgaratanamālassa udikkhanti sadevakā
         kammena taṃ kiṇitvāna ratanaṃ tumhe pilandhathāti.
      Bhante nāgasena katamaṃ buddhassa bhagavato sabbāpaṇanti.
  Sabbāpaṇaṃ kho mahārāja bhagavato navaṅgaṃ buddhassa vacanaṃ sārīrikāni
  paribhoganiyāni cetiyāni saṃgharatanañca. Sabbāpaṇe kho mahārāja
  bhagavato jātisampatti pasāritā bhogasampatti pasāritā āyusampatti
  pasāritā ārogyasampatti pasāritā vaṇṇasampatti pasāritā
  paññāsampatti pasāritā ñāṇasampatti pasāritā mānussikasampatti
  pasāritā dibbasampatti pasāritā nibbānasampatti pasāritā.
  Tattha ye sampattiṃ icchanti te kammamūlaṃ datvā patthitapatthitaṃ
  sampattiṃ kīṇanti keci sīlasamādānena kīṇanti keci uposathakammena
  kīṇanti appamattakena pi kammamūlena upādāyupādāya sampattiyo
  labhanti. Yathā mahārāja āpaṇikassa āpaṇe tilamuggamāse
-------------------------------------------------
[page 427]
  Parittenapi taṇḍulamuggamāsakenapi mūlena upādāyupādāya bhaṇḍaṃ
  labhanti evameva kho mahārāja bhagavato sabbāpaṇe appamattakenapi
  kammamūlena upādāyupādāya sampattiyo labhanti. Tatretaṃ vuccati
  buddhassa bhagavato sabbāpaṇanti.
         Āyu ārogitaṃ vaṇṇaṃ saggaṃ uccākulīnakaṃ
         asaṃkhatañca amataṃ atthi sabbāpaṇe jine
         appakena bahukenāpi kammamūlena labhati
         saddhāmūlena kīṇitvā samiddhā hotha bhikkhavoti.
      Bhagavato kho mahārāja dhammanagare evarūpā janā paṭivasanti.
  Dhammakathikā vinayadharā suttantikā ābhidhammikā jātakabhaṇikā dīghabhaṇikā
  majjhimabhaṇikā saṃyuttabhaṇikā aṅguttarabhaṇikā khuddakabhaṇikā sīlasampannā
  samādhisampannā paññāsampannā bojjhaṅgabhāvanāratā
  vipassakā sadatthamanuyuttā sosānikā palālapuñjakā yathāsanthatikā
  abbhokāsikā rukkhamūlavāsino nesajjikā upayuttā paṭipannakā phalaṭṭhā
  sekkhā phalasamaṅgino sotāpannā sakadāgāmī anāgāmī arahantā
  tevijjā chaḷabhiññā iddhimanto paññāpāramigatā
  satipaṭṭhānasammappadhānaiddhipādaindriyabalabojjhaṅgamaggapavarajhānavimokkharūpā-
  rūpasaññāsantisukhavihārasuññatānimittāppaṇihitāneñjaupekkhāphala-
  samāpattikusalā tehi janehi ākulaṃ samākulaṃ ākiṇṇaṃ samākiṇṇaṃ
  naḷavanasaralavanamiva dhammanagaraṃ ahosi. Bhavatiha
-------------------------------------------------
[page 428]
         Vītarāgā vītadosā vītamohā anāsavā
         vītataṇhā anādānā dhammanagare vasanti te.
         Ārakā ṭhitā vimuttā jhāyino lūkhacīvarā
         araññāsādamunayo dhammanagare vasanti te.
         Nesajjikā sosānikā athopi ṭhānacaṅkamā
         paṃsukūladharā sabbe dhammanagare vasanti te.
         Ticīvaradharā santā dhammakhandhacatutthakā
         ratā ekāsane viññū dhammanagare vasanti te.
         Appicchā dhutaṅge samācārā nipuṇā lahukavuttino
         lābhālābhe ca santuṭṭhā dhammanagare vasanti te.
         Jhāyijjhānaratā dhīrā santacittā samāhitā
         ākiñcaññaṃ paṭṭhayānā dhammanagare vasanti te.
         Paṭipannakā phalaṭṭhā ca sekkhā phalasamaṅgino
         āsiṃsakā uttamatthaṃ dhammanagare vasanti te.
         Sotāpannā ca vimalā sakadāgāmino ca ye
         anāgāmī arahanto dhammanagare vasanti te.
         Satipaṭṭhānakusalā bojjhaṅgabhāvane ratā
         vipassakā bahū sabbe dhammanagare vasanti te.
         Iddhipādesu kusalā samādhibhāvane ratā
         sammappadhānamanuyuttā dhammanagare vasanti te.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 429]
         Abhiññā pāramippattā pītiyā gocare ratā
         antalikkhamhi caraṇā dhammanagare vasanti te.
         Okkhittacakkhū mitabhāṇī guttadvārā susaṃvutā
         sudantā uttamadhammā dhammanagare vasanti te.
         Tevijjā chaḷabhiññā ca iddhiyā pāramiṃ gatā
         paññāya pāramippattā dhammanagare vasanti teti.
      Ye te mahārāja bhikkhū aparimitañāṇavaradharā asaṅgā atuliyā
  guṇayasadharā siridharā baladharā javadharā iddhidharā pabhāsadharā matidharā
  vimuttidharā dhammadharā dhammacakkadharā paññāpāramiṃ gatā evarūpā
  kho mahārāja bhikkhū bhagavato dhammanagare dhammasenāpatinoti vuccanti.
  Ye pana te mahārāja bhikkhū iddhimanto adhigatapaṭisambhidā
  pattavesārajjā guṇadharā dūrāsadā anālambacarā sāgaramahindharakampakā
  candimasuriyaparimajjakā vikubbanamadhiṭṭhānamahābhinīhārakusalā iddhipāramiṃ
  gatā evarūpā kho mahārāja bhikkhū bhagavato dhammanagare purohitāti
  vuccanti. Ye pana te mahārāja bhikkhū dhutaṅgamanugatā appicchā
  nipakā santuṭṭhā viññattimanesanajigucchakā piṇḍāya sapadānacārino
  rūpasaddagandharasaphoṭṭhabbāṅgeṭṭhitā bhamarāva gandhamanughāyitvā
  vivittakānanaṃ pavisanti kāyesu ca jīvitesu ca anapekkhā
  arahattamanuppattā dhutaṅgaguṇe anikkhittā evarūpā kho mahārāja bhikkhū
  bhagavato dhammanagare dhammakkhadassāti vuccanti. Ye pana te
  mahārāja bhikkhū parisuddhā vimalā nikkilesā cutūpapātakusalā
-------------------------------------------------
[page 430]
  Dibbacakkhumhi pāramiṃ gatā evarūpā kho mahārāja bhagavato dhammanagare
  jotakāti vuccanti. Ye pana te mahārāja bhikkhū bahussutā
  āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā sithiladhanitadīgharassānu-
  kathūlāndhakkharaupakkharaparicchedakusalā navaṅgasāsanadharā evarūpā kho
  mahārāja bhikkhū bhagavato dhammanagare dhammarakjāti vuccanti.
  Ye pana te mahārāja bhikkhū vinayaññū vinayakovidā nidānavatthukusalā
  āpattianāpattigarulahukasatekicchāatekicchāvuṭṭhānadesanāniggaha-
  paṭikammaosāraṇanissāraṇapaṭisāraṇakusalā vinaye pāramiṃ gatā
  evarūpā kho mahārāja bhikkhū bhagavato dhammanagare rūpadakkhāti
  vuccanti. Ye pana te mahārāja bhikkhū vimuttivarakusumamālabaddhā
  varamahagghabhāvamanuppattā bahujanakantābhipatthitā evarūpā kho
  mahārāja bhikkhū bhagavato dhammanagare pupphāpaṇikāti vuccanti. Ye pana
  te mahārāja bhikkhū catusaccābhisamayapaṭividdhā diṭṭhasaccā viññātasāsanā
  catūsu sāmaññavaraphalesu chinnavimativicikicchā paṭiladdhaphalasukhā
  aññesampi paṭipannānaṃ te phale saṃvibhajanti evarūpā kho mahārāja
  bhikkhū bhagavato dhammanagare phalāpaṇikāti vuccanti. Ye pana te
  mahārāja bhikkhū sīlavarasurabhigandhābbhantarabāhiramanulittā
  anekavividhabahuguṇadharā kilesamaladuggandhavidhamakā evarūpā kho mahārāja bhikkhū
  bhagavato dhammanagare gandhāpaṇikāti vuccanti. Ye pana te mahārāja
  bhikkhū dhammakāmā piyasamudāhārā abhidhamme abhivinaye uḷārapāmojjā
  ekāsanikā gagaṇagatā araññagatā rukkhamūlagatā suññāgāragatā
-------------------------------------------------
[page 431]
  Dhammavaraguṇarasaṃ pivanti kāyena vācāya manasā dhammavararasamogāḷhā
  adhimattapaṭibhāṇā dhammesu dhammesanapaṭipannā ito ca ito ca
  yattha yattha sandhāpakā appicchakathā santuṭṭhikathā pavivekakathā
  asaṃsaggakathā viriyārambhakathā sīlakathā samādhikathā paññākathā
  vimuttikathā vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā tattha tattha gantvā taṃ taṃ
  kathārasaṃ pivanti evarūpā kho mahārāja bhikkhū bhagavato dhammanagare
  pipāsā soṇḍāti vuccanti. Ye pana te mahārāja bhikkhū
  purimamajjhimapacchimayāme jāgariyānuyogamanuyuttā nisajjāṭhānacaṅkamehi rattiṃ
  atikkāmenti sadatthapasutā bhāvanānuyogamanuyuttā kilesapaṭibāhanāya
  evarūpā kho mahārāja bhikkhū bhagavato dhammanagare nagararakkhāti
  vuccanti. Ye pana te mahārāja bhikkhū navaṅgaṃ buddhavacanaṃ atthato
  ca byañjanato ca nayato ca kāraṇato ca hetuto ca udāharaṇato
  ca vācenti anuvācenti bhāsanti anubhāsanti evarūpā kho
  mahārāja bhikkhū bhagavato dhammanagare dhammāpaṇikāti vuccanti. Ye pana
  te mahārāja bhikkhū bhagavato dhammaratanabhogena āgamapariyattisutabhogena
  bhogino dhanino niddiṭṭhasarabyañjanalakkhaṇapaṭivedhā vipassanāviññū
  pharaṇā evarūpā kho mahārāja bhikkhū bhagavato dhammanagare
  dhammaseṭṭhinoti vuccanti. Ye pana te mahārāja bhikkhū
  uḷāradesanapaṭivedhā ārammaṇapariciṇṇavibhattiniddesā sikkhāguṇapāramigatā
  evarūpā kho mahārāja bhikkhū bhagavato dhammanagare issaradhammikāti
  vuccanti. Evaṃ suvibhattaṃ kho mahārāja bhagavato dhammanagaraṃ evaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 432]
  Sumāpitaṃ evaṃ suvihitaṃ evaṃ suparipūritaṃ evaṃ suvavatthāpitaṃ evaṃ
  sugopitaṃ evaṃ surakkhitaṃ evaṃ apasayhaṃ paccatthikehi paccāmittehi.
  Iminā mahārāja kāraṇena iminā hetunā iminā nayena iminā
  anumānena ñātabbaṃ atthi so bhagavāti. Bhavatiha
      kammena sukataṃ nagaraṃ jānanti yadi pana so vaḍḍhakī kālakato
  hoti evaṃ ciraṃ kālaṃ parinibbutaṃpi santaṃ buddhasubhāsitaṃ kammena
  pāliyā jānanti pubbācariyāti.
      Bhante nāgasena dhammanagaraṃ taṃ māpetuṃ sakkā tumhādisehi
  aññehi ca balappattehi bhikkhūhi. Dukkaraṃ bhante nāgasena etena
  kāraṇena saddahituṃ atthi so bhagavāti. Bhiyyo upamaṃ karohīti.
  Yathā mahārāja mahāsamuddo anupubbagambhīro aparimitabālukajaladharo
  timitimiṅgalasamākulo nāgasupaṇṇadānavaniketo varuṇālayo
  sāgaramappameyyo anekajaṅgalarāmalakkhaṇapāratiṇṇo taraṅgasuravikkhobhita-
  maṇḍitamodakarāsī anekadundubhigītanithutameghaninnādaṃ nadito
  tumulasubhīmaghoso ālayaparālayo ṭhānuttamaggapuggalānaṃ paññānidassanena
  vipulamantakāya pāpinavāriyaparigaḷanaṃ nāgayakkhavikkhobhitasalilo
  sampattiyattamaggavejayantiyaekaravātayuttā kālikavaṅgikaubhitthasamuṭṭhito
  catuguṇadānavālayo anekadīpasatasahassagirisikharapaggahitagivasiro
  pubbadevatālayo vā dijagaṇākiṇṇo anekanāvāvāṇijasaṃghavicittataraṅga-
  toyapaṇḍarasikharavippharavippakiṇṇo pheṇumālinīmahodadhimahaṇṇavo
  anālayapatiṭṭhiyo kepukapātālanarakapadahimukhapubbadho
-------------------------------------------------
[page 433]
  Aṭṭhācchariyābbhūtadhammasamannāgato pahūtaratanavicitto macchakacchapasañchanno
  ummitaraṅgamālādīpakarapaṭṭanavicitto. Tasmiṃ khalu mahārāja
  mahāsamudde evarūpā macchā paṭivasanti sikhisaṃsavanamakapathitaṃpakulavala-
  imuñjitakaraveladikaropikahilakākahilajalakāpalāsādiyārohitakamasanacandana-
  arigaṇhakamusalakamuṭṭhikatilamaṇihatthikāssikatilataṇḍulakamalakamānussaka-
  sarabhasoṇḍikamaṇḍikamakaratimitimiṅgalā. Ete ca mahārāja aññe ca
  anekavidhā mahāsamudde paṭivasanti. Atha paneko mahāmaccho
  attano thāmabalaṃ sampassamāno mahāsamuddaṃ khobheyya pakhobheyya
  sampakhobheyya ummivegañca dasseyya. Tamenaṃ tīramanugatā janā
  ummivegaṃ passitvā anumānena jānanti mahanto vata bho
  mahāmaccho bhavissati yathadidaṃ ummivegā paṭidissantīti. Evameva kho
  mahārāja ayaṃpi lokasamuddo anupubbagambhīro aparimitarāgadosamohajaladharo
  bālaputhujjanasamākulo kilesasaṃghāṭajaṭiko diṭṭhijālaparikkhitto
  taṇhāsotamanusandamāno mānadhajasamussito tividhaggiāditto
  añāṇamandakārapihito luddhasāhasiko ekacakkhu viya bhīruko
  andhajanamāsayo sukataṃ dukkaṭaṃ yasavantaṃ ayasavantaṃ puññavantaṃ
  apuññavantaṃ tejavantaṃ atejavantaṃ guṇavantaṃ aguṇavantaṃ vuddhivantaṃ
  avuddhivantaṃ paññavantaṃ apaññavantaṃ buddhajanaṃ abuddhajanaṃ sabbesaṃ sattānaṃ
  āsayo. Tasmiṃ kho mahārāja lokasamudde evarūpā janā
  paṭivasanti puthū khattiyā brāhmaṇā vessā suddā ājīvakā
-------------------------------------------------
[page 434]
  Paṇḍaraṅgā paribbājakā nigaṇṭhā buddhasāvakā gotamakā ekasikkhā
  dvedhammikā thandilakaseyyā ekapassesāyikā rajojalladharā
  kaṇṭhapassiyikā udakorohakā ajapati sunakhapati gopati acelakā tāpasā.
  Oṭṭhagoṇagadrabhājapasumahisarrusasaeṇeyyavarāharohitapasatabheraṇḍa-
  sākhāmigapalāsādagavajagajā rājahaṃsakoñcamayurakaravīkavāyasakuṇālacākavāka-
  sukasālikakokilakapotaukkusākuḷīrakapokkharasātakatilambakapupphaka-
  jīvañjīvakanandikamahākālabhimasenarohiccavinagulikururisenatittirakalijali-
  kukkusāvaṭakaulukaronahiāṭācelagittakādepanalakā ca sakuṇā. Ete
  ca mahārāja aññe ca ye keci sattā sattanikāyapariyāpannā sabbe
  te lokabaddhā lokasamudde antogatā lokasamudde parivattanti.
  Atha pana mahārāja so bhagavā loke sañjāto nibbatto abhinibbatto
  dasasahassalokadhātuṃ kampayitvā sabbaññutañāṇamanāvaraṇaṃ anuttaraṃ
  ñāṇaṃ passitvā dhammumminā sadevakaṃ lokaṃ khobhesi pakhobhesi
  dhammummidhammavīcidhammokkadhammadīpadhammadhajadhammaketuṃ ussāpesi samussāpesi.
  Katamaṃ pana taṃ mahārāja bhagavato dhammummivegaṃ yāya ummiyā so
  bhagavā sadevakaṃ lokaṃ khobhesi pakhobhesi tāresi patāresi ussāpesi
  samussāpesi pālesi anupālesi dhāresi ādhāresi ussāsesi
  anussāsesi. Sabbe satte bhayā parimocesi sabbaduggatiyā
  mocesi pamocesi parimocesi vimocesi. Abhayaṃ desi amataṃ
  desi agadaṃ desi. Sabbosathaṃ desi paramaṃ desi khemabhūmiṃ
  sampāpesi. Amalaṃ karosi vimalaṃ karosi nimmalaṃ karosi socesi
-------------------------------------------------
[page 435]
  Visocesi paṇḍaraṃ karosi damesi samesi upasamesi. Sabbapaṭisambhidāgame
  vimokkhepi arahattepi patiṭṭhapesi. Idaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasaccanti
  kho mahārāja bhagavā kathayamāno sadevakaṃ lokaṃ khobhesi
  dhammummidhammavīciṃ dassesi. Bhagavato kho mahārāja dhammummivegaṃ
  disvā keci sotāpattiphalaṃ sacchikaronti. Keci sakadāgāmiphalaṃ
  keci anāgāmiphalaṃ sacchikaronti. Keci sīlaṃ samādiyanti. Keci
  upāsake paṭidesenti. Keci saddhāmattaṃ pemamattaṃ paṭilabhanti.
  Yathā mahārāja bhagavā tāvatiṃsabhavane nisinno paṇḍukambalasilāyaṃ
  dukkhasaccaṃ kathesi. Tattha bhagavato dhammummivegena asītidevatākoṭīnaṃ
  virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi yaṃ kiñci samudayadhammaṃ sabbantaṃ
  nirodha dhammanti. Ye te pana mahārāja bhagavato dhammummivegaṃ
  disvā niṭṭhaṃ gacchanti te evarūpaṃ uḷāraṃ visesaṃ paṭilabhanti.
  Samudayasaccaṃ kho mahārāja bhagavā kathayamāno sadevakaṃ lokaṃ khobhesi
  pakhobhesi sampakhobhesi dhammummidhammavīciṃ ussāpesi samussāpesi.
  Yathā mahārāja bhagavā pāsāṇakacetiye nisinno samudayasaccaṃ
  kathesi. Tatra bhagavato dhammummivegena cuddasapāṇakoṭīnaṃ virajaṃ
  vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ udapādi yaṃ kiñci samudayadhammaṃ sabbantaṃ
  nirodhadhammanti. Ye pana te mahārāja bhagavato dhammummivegaṃ
  disvā niṭṭhaṃ gacchanti te evarūpaṃ uḷāraṃ visesaṃ paṭilabhanti.
  Nirodhasaccaṃ kho mahārāja bhagavā kathayamāno sadevakaṃ lokaṃ khobhesi
  pakhobhesi sampakhobhesi dhammummidhammavīciṃ dassesi. Yathā mahārāja
-------------------------------------------------
[page 436]
  Bhagavā tāvatiṃsabhavanato orohitvā nirodhasaccaṃ kathesi. Tatrāpi
  bhagavato dhammummivegena naramarūnaṃ tiṃsakoṭīnaṃ virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhuṃ
  udapādi yaṃ kiñci samudayadhammaṃ sabbantaṃ nirodhadhammanti. Ye pana
  te mahārāja bhagavato dhammummivegaṃ disvā niṭṭhaṃ gacchanti te
  evarūpaṃ uḷāraṃ visesaṃ adhigacchanti. Yepi te mahārāja etarahi
  bhagavato sāvakā ghātitabandhanā hatarāgadosamohā pahīnakilesā
  suddhamanā suvisuddhamanā tiṇṇamanā suvitiṇṇamanā sacchikatā
  suvisacchikatā arahattamanuppattā tepi bhagavato dhammummivegena
  santipadamanuttaraṃ pattā. Imināpi mahārāja kāraṇena iminā nayena
  iminā anumānena ñātabbaṃ atthi so bhagavāti. Bhavatiha
         anumānena jānantiṃ ummiṃ disvāna sāgare
         yathāyaṃ dissate ummi mahanto so bhavissati
         tatheva buddhaṃ sokanudaṃ sabbattha aparājitaṃ
         taṇhakkhayamanuppattaṃ bhava saṃsāra mocanaṃ
         anumānena ñātabbaṃ ummiṃ disvā sadevake
         yathā dhammummivipphāro aggo buddho bhavissatīti.
      Bhante nāgasena sutapubbaṃ amhehi bāhirakāpi isayo sadevakaṃ
  lokaṃ kampenti pakampenti sampakampenti iddhivegañca dassenti.
  Dukkaraṃ bhante nāgasena etena kāraṇena saddahituṃ atthi buddhoti.
  Bhiyyo upamaṃ karohīti. Yathā mahārāja himavā pabbatarājā
  naggauggaselasikharapabbhārakūṭamālako
-------------------------------------------------
[page 437]
  Ajapathasaṃkupathavalapathapettapathatatthittho ca chaddantakulaniketo aññāṇagottarakalama-
  dumamālakoṭakanikaviṇṇavasilaghāpaṇānucarito anekanānādumalatāvitthibhapaccuggato
  oṇatavithitaphalitadumo sukhasītalagirikandaratanusaliladhāraṇānukulo
  anekadhātusattavinaddhamaṇḍitagato gandhabbadevagaṇakilikāvāsito
  vijjādharadijavihaṅgānilambaramanuciṇṇo supaṇṇamahoragāsurakumbhaṇḍadānavālayo
  ghorabhīmadhumākāraṇadaṭṭhadiṭṭhiphuṭṭhavātāvisauragamāsīvisānamālayo
  nānosatha agadabhesajjamūlakaphalasamākulo jātivaracandana-
  aggalūtagarakālānusāritālīsakāleyyapattharasakutthabyagghanakhāhiriponasirabaddha-
  muttakasañchanno kotumbaravarelasabbagandhānamākaro tikūṭagirikūṭa
  kelāsasumanacitrakūṭayugandharagirikūṭapavārito nīlasunīlapalaghanoghokālameghariva
  abbhuggato paripuṇṇo balāhako viya kāḷapakkhadivasupagato
  varaseṭṭhapurakaṇhāñjanānuvilitto viya nāgarājā nīlajaladharo viya
  sāvaṇṇatāya ākāsatimiro viya accuggato himavantapabbatarājā. Atha naṃ
  duramāgatajanā sutapubbamadassāvino himavantapabbatarājaṃ passitvā
  selakūṭagirikūṭatimirā viya accuggataṃ anekanānādumalatāvajaṅghakāvinaddhaṃ
  disvā anumānena jānanti himavā vata eso pabbatarājā bhavissati
  yathāyaṃ kūṭapabbatatimiro viya paṭidissatīti. Evameva kho mahārāja tassa
  bhagavato dhammapabbato anekajjhānadhammaphalāarammaṇavinaddho
  suññatānimittāppaṇihitakūṭapabbhāramālayo dhammakathikavinayadharasuttantika-
  ābhidhammikātiphalo arahattakusalaniketo sotāpaṭipannakaphalaṭṭhakasekkhaphala-
  samaṅgīsotāpannānucarito
-------------------------------------------------
[page 438]
  Ghorarūpauggatadūrāsanalūkhacīvaradharapiṇḍapātikāppicchadhutaṅgasamādhārāasevita-
  nisevito tevijjachaḷabhiññādhigatapaṭisambhidappattavesārajjapaññādipāramigatāna-
  mālayo vamanavirecanāgadabhesajjasabbosathamāyupālanamākiṇṇo sīlacandanasamatha-
  agadapaññātagarasatipaṭṭhānarasasantuṭṭhavisuddhasabbagandhānamākaro satipaṭṭhāna-
  sammappadhānaindriyabalabojjhaṅgamaggavarapabbataparivārito
  accugtato arajaṃ virajaṃ amalaṃ vimalaṃ visuddhaṃ suvisuddhaṃ paṇḍaraṃ
  avajjaṃ anavajjaṃ asaṃkhataṃ amadananimmadanapipāsavinayadarathamupasamāalayasamugghāṭa-
  nandikkhayadosakkhayamohakkhayaupadhikkhayataṇhakkhayavirāga-
  nirodhājātājarāmaraṇasukhasītibhāvaṃ nibbānaṃ abhayakāraṇaṃ
  kugaṇīparappavādānamabhibhuyya abbhuggato dhammāccuggato apacito
  abhipūjitasakkata sakkamānito. Tassa bhagavato dhammapabbatoti imināpi
  mahārāja kāraṇena iminā anumānena ñātabbaṃ tassa bhagavato
  seṭṭhabhāvaṃ adantadamanandanaṃ so bhagavā arahaṃ sammā sambuddho
  bhavissatīti. Yatra hi nāma evaṃ ciraparinibbutassāpi acalaṃ akampanīyaṃ
  suvitthārakaṃ sāsanaṃ caranti. Bhavatiha
         anumānena jānanti disvā accuggataṃ giriṃ
         yathā accuggato eso himavā so bhavissati
         tatheva disvā dhammagiriṃ sītibhūtaṃ nirūpadhiṃ
         accuggataṃ bhagavato acalaṃ supatiṭṭhitaṃ
         anumānena ñātabbaṃ disvāna dhammapabbataṃ
         yathā hi so mahāvīro aggo buddho bhavissatīti.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 439]
      Bhante nāgasena sati bāhirakesu pāvacanesu anekavidhena
  lokasamuddaṃpi dassenti dhammagiriṃpi dassenti. Dukkaraṃ bhante nāgasena
  etena kāraṇena saddahituṃ atthi buddhoti. Bhiyyo upamaṃ
  karohīti. Yathā pana mahārāja catudīpako nāma mahāmegho
  mahatimahāmeghavasena sammāvassanto khobhento piṇḍivaṭaṃsake
  ākāsāghumāyusamāhitapaccuggate pādape anileritāni pādapagaṇāni naccanti
  kusumāvātā papatanti ciraddhakusumasita vātagahitā pharanti nadanti
  girinadiyo sahassānapaṭalakhobhitarukkhattakatā vānarā utrāsātamigasaṃghā
  uggatavippasitā ākāsagatā dijagaṇā tahiṃ tahiṃ balāhakapantiyo
  dissanti nīlameghantare abhinadanti uḷārā āmoditā varāhakapotakā
  kalorupotā kuñcanādaṃ abhinandanti naccanti girisikharamuddhanigatā
  sikhiṇḍino abhinandanti devā vassanti devā taḷāyanti devā
  gatajāyanti indadhanū vijjulatā balāhakatalaṭhitā megho aghasitagamano
  yathā ākāsabhavanagatimālutakhittasatapaṭalasahassā patalameghanīlapītalohita-
  odātamañjeṭṭhataruṇavaṇṇā nadipadaṇḍadharaṇidhanusañhitā
  susitikaravipphalakāyavighutthadundubhisarasadisatulyaghosasampannabalāhakaparipuṇṇamegha-
  maṇḍaleneva jaladharasatasaṅkulā vijjusatasaṅkulā tamenaṃ aññe disvā
  jhāyino jānanti. Sobbhasarakandaraupadarabilaudapānataḷākapokkharaṇiyo
  sampuṇṇavassasalilāasitodakā mahāpaṭhavī kalala kaddamupagatā suviruḷhā
  pādapalatā kālameghariva udakaṃ āmoditapamodite varāhasaṃghe parihīnatame
  ca suvaṇṇādike ca kasakappakadhuvappasatthena
-------------------------------------------------
[page 440]
  Nīhataharitasaddalajagatipaṭhaviṃ passitvā anumānena jānanti mahā vata bho megho
  abhippavuṭṭho bhavissati yathāyaṃ paṭhavī sītatalanibbutoti evameva kho
  mahārāja bhagavā mahādhammameghena abhivassamāno amatavasseneva
  lokaṃ tappayanto paritappayanato paṭipannakaphalasamaṅgino mānasaṃ
  bodhāya niyatamanubodhayanto dhammavaraṃ vijjutaṃ dassento avajjaṃ virajaṃ
  vimalaṃ pītimuppādayanto rāgadosamohamānadiṭṭhisaṃghāṭajaṭitajaṭitaṃ
  vidhamayanto nandilataṃ maddayanto byāharanto dhammavaraṃ
  kugaṇiparappavādānaṃ bhañjayanto parasamayaṃ vināsayanto sakasamayaṃ dīpayanto
  anantanayūpetaṃ dhammavaradharaṃ dhārayanto ālokamanudassayanto assāsaṃ
  dento sattānaṃ lokaṃ tārayanto sabbavidūvibhāvīnaṃ vedayanto
  aggayasadhammakāmānaṃ nandayanto sampattiyasakāmānaṃ modayanto
  sabbakilesabandhanato tārayanto sabbabhavakantārā nibbāpento
  sabbadarathapariḷāhānaṃ khayaṃ pāpento vimuttirasaṃ seṭṭhamhi pāpayanto
  khemantabhūmiṃ āropayanto sabbākusaladhamme pidahanto saggupāyaṃ
  vivaranto saggapathaṃ sampattimanupādayanto amataṃ pāpayanto arahattehi
  cittaṃ vimocayanto pavaradhammameghena sadevakaṃ lokaṃ tappesi. Atha
  panāyaṃ janatā āmoditapamoditā hasitapahasitā veditapaveditā
  tuṭṭhasumanamānasā bhūtasuddhavirajapaṇḍaravimuttimānasā nibbutā tividhaggiṃ
  maddeti pariḷāhānujalakamattakāmaṃ sabbabhavagativantaṃ suddhaṃ virajaṃ anaṅgaṇaṃ
  amataṃ nibbānaṃ garukataṃ māhitaṃ mānitaṃ pūjitaṃ abhivanditaṃ
  haṭṭhatuṭṭhaudaggāamoditajanataṃ disvā anumānena ñātabbaṃ oḷāro so
-------------------------------------------------
[page 441]
  Bhagavā bhavissati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhoti. Bhavatiha
         nibbutaṃ paṭhaviṃ disvā harituppatti mahodakaṃ
         anumānena jānanti mahāmeghena nibbutaṃ
         tathevimaṃ janaṃ disvā āmoditappamoditaṃ
         anumānena ñātabbaṃ dhammameghena tappitanti.
      Subuddho bhante nāgasena bhagavatā dhammamegho avijjāpi
  dhuvavuṭṭhi anuvattati bhiyyo buddhabalaṃ dassehi hetunā kāraṇenāti.
  Yathā mahārāja gajarājā varapavaro sattubbedho navāyāmo
  dasapariṇāho dharaṇigaṇako setakkho setavālo setanakhasikharo
  setacchattakubbhalakkhaṇavimānantaro paṇḍaravimalasetābbhugaṇasadisa-
  sannikāso paripuṇṇapīnānupubbasujāto ghaṇaruciraghaṇavattapīnapāso
  supāruabhirūpadassanatappaṇeyyo kamalasatasupatiṭṭho āyatanavisālaputhula-
  vitthiṇṇasattappatittho brahātaruṇaparamānugtato girikūṭasadisasannikāso.
  Dumadummaṃ ca vidhaṃsanto kappayanto ca supatthigaruhante
  dharaṇitalaṃ sakaṃ calaṃ kamati madamattatipāpapabhinno mātaṅgo.
  Īsādantavilāsabalaubbalhabalavātibalavā dūrāsadaduppaṭisayho
  duṭṭhatikkhakharakaṭukāniserittadhātukopi dāruṇo vithitthāni kusalo sakamāsayaṃ
  pajahitvā gocarāyamanugacchanto ubhatosandamānatiṇalatādumapādapagaṇe
  ca samūlaṃ matthayanto jito ca samavithinatajaṭajaṭaveḷuyo mānapāvasanto
  savalasakabhakkhamesamāno kānanavanasikharavipariyapāvane. Atha
-------------------------------------------------
[page 442]
  Pana yo koci manusso taṃ passeyya tassa gajarājavarassa gatamaggo
  samūlamatthitabhaggadharaṇiruhadumapādapagaṇe ca tiṇalatāvanagumbagacchaveḷuyo
  ca samūlamatthitāyo tahaṃ tahaṃ puñjakatasākharāsipadumalatātiṇānaṃ ākulatā
  kampitāyo ca saṃpanapatthitakoṭiyo laggitāni anudissanti
  dharaṇiruhapādapatte abhimārapadarāssatthabyājjhāghāṭakā kapitthasamūlamatthite
  ca pādapagaṇena kaṇerukasaṃghaparivārito raṃsi tathā nagaramigapalāsito
  ca so gajarājā tassa gajarājavarapavaradurāsadassa
  āyātapādamanumajjānukuṭitā pādalekhā gajapavaralakkhaṇamupetaṃ
  piyamanuññasabbalakkhaṇavicittaṃ mahantaṃ hatthipadaṃ passitvā anumānena jānāti
  mahanto vata bho gajarājā bhavissati yathā yathā tassa padavitikkamo
  paññāyatīti. Evameva kho mahārāja so bhagavā tathānāgo
  tathāsīho tathānisabho tathādanto tathāsanto tathāsacco
  tathākhanti tathāadhiṭṭhāno tathāparakkamo tathādhiti tathāviriyo
  tathāñāṇī tathāvasī tathājuti tathāsukho tathāyaso tathātejo
  tathāvimutto tathāsaṅghī tathāgaṇī tathāisi tathāmuni tathāguru tathārājā
  tathādevo tathāindo tathāsakko tathābrahmā tathāsabbadhammasamannāgato
  tathāparitto tathātāṇo tathāleṇo tathāsaraṇo
  tathāpatiṭṭho tathāparāyano tathāvidito tathāsaṃvidito tathābuddho
  tathākevalabuddhadhammasamannāgato tathākāruṇiko tathāāneñjo
  tathāsabbadado tathākusalo. Bhagavā rammapuravaraṃ kapilavatthuṃ chaḍḍayitvā
  catudīpamekachattapatte padasasatamatireke sattavidharatanāni sakamāsayaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 443]
  Jahitvā dasabalo sakabhakkhadhammadharāsaṃkhatamesamāno suññavivittakānanamanupavisitvā
  vijjāñāṇavarapavaramesamāno ca odhunanto rāgarajareṇuṃ
  dhamanto mohamadamānamakkhasaṃsayaṃ samūhanto chindanto sabbapāsakakudiṭṭhakāmalataṃ
  nandilataṃ padālayanto kodhamānaṃ ghāṭayanto taṇhāsotasaritaṃ
  tārayanto vitakkamanaṃ chindanto kumaggaṃ pidahanto
  vivaranto amatamanuttaraṃ dukkhato satte parimocento
  aṭṭhaṅgikasivamaggamanuttarena dhammanagaraṃ pavisitvā sabbaññutañāṇapavaramanuttaraṃ
  pāpuṇitvā satta sambojjhaṅgavarapadāni dassesi. Tāni mahārāja
  bhagavato pādāni āyatanāni suāyatanāni vitthatāni suvibhattāni
  anūnāni parikiṇṇāni abbhokiṇṇāni samantāni sabbalakkhaṇasamannāgatāni
  cittāni vicittāni dassanīyāni sudassanīyāni pemanīyāni
  tosanīyāni vedanīyāni nandanīyāni sotthikaraṇīyāni khemakaraṇīyāni
  abhayakaraṇīyāni assāsakaraṇīyāni aparitāsakaraṇīyāni pītikaraṇīyāni
  pāmojjakaraṇīyāni ekaggakaraṇīyāni bhāvanīyāni subhāvanīyāni sukhadadāni
  sītadadāni yasadadāni baladadāni āyudadāni vaṇṇadadāni bhogadadāni
  kāmadadāni patthitadadāni sabbadadāni titthiyagaṇā ye mahiyaṃ te
  sabbe abhibhuyya evarūpāni buddhassa padāni satthupadāni sīhapadāni
  nāgapadāni usabhapadāni nisabhapadāni vasabhapadāni yakkhapadāni rājapadāni
  indapadāni devapadāni sakkapadāni purindadapadāni brahmapadāni
  santipadāni gaṇipadāni isipadāni munipadāni jinapadāni varapadāni
  uttamapadāni aggapadāni jeṭṭhapadāni amatapadāni vimuttapadāni
-------------------------------------------------
[page 444]
  Arahantapadāni buddhapadāni sambuddhapadāni dassenti. Iminā
  mahārāja kāraṇena iminā hetunā iminā anumānena ñātabbaṃ
  uḷāro so bhagavā bhavissati yathā tassa padāni paññāyantīti.
      Acchariyaṃ bhante nāgasena abbhūtaṃ bhante nāgasena gajarājassa
  kho bhante nāgasena yāni padāni na ciraṃ tiṭṭhanti cha pañca
  māsāni tiṭṭhanti tato paraṃ vinassanti. Imāni kho pana bhante
  nāgasena bhagavato padāni yāvāyaṃ loko tiṭṭhati yāvajjatanā
  paṭipajjissanti tāni ca buddhapadāni paññāyissantīti. Bhiyyo
  buddhabalaṃ dīpehi hetunā kāraṇenāti. Yathā mahārāja sīho
  migarājā vigatabhayabheravo acchambhito anibbhito vigatalomahaṃso
  parapāṇaruhiramigabhakkho upacittavipulanibbattagato dāḍhiatibalipubbaḍḍhakāyo
  anunnatamanonataparipuṇṇamaruhirakesarī sukesarī abhijātivanto durāsado
  dupasayho adhipatimigābhibhū catuvalañjanānugatikapadavitikkamagatisiddhiupadhita-
  sarīrakalaṅkaṇeyyo thāmāsadisaviriyauggatabalo alīnapaṭisallino
  asayaniketo suññavivittavanapavanasevī paṇḍaravimalasetābbhughanasadisa-
  rajaṭapattasadisasannikāso sāyaṇhasamaye āsayā abhinikkhamitvā
  catuddisaṃ vilokayitvā mahantaṃ vilinabhāvamupagantvā parapāṇamaviheṭhayanto
  dharaṇitalasakagaṇaṃ hāsayanto sīhanādamabhinadati. Abhinadite
  ca pana migaraññe udakāsayadarīsayavilāsayagūhāsayavanāsayāakāsagagaṇapāragatāpi
  te vihaṅgā tattheva bhītabhītā patanti. Atha pana
  yo koci passeyya te migasaṃghe āleyyanabhantamubbiggavitthitamānase
-------------------------------------------------
[page 445]
  Sotthikāravipulitabhantavalitabuddhitavedanā aṭhitāniyāme māluteritasalilasandena
  patamāne disavidisasakaṭṭhāne vihajjamāne disvā anumānena
  jānāti sīho nu kho migarājā vinadanto bhavissati yathāpiyime
  kumigā bhantamubbiggahasitavitthatamānasāti. Evameva kho mahārāja so
  bhagavā vigatabhayabheravo acchambhitamubbiggalomahaṃso
  atulaguṇānantaguṇadhitipāramigato saraṇagamanassāsako acalāsadisasuvimuttipaṇḍara-
  sabbaññutañāṇamanāvaraṇañāṇo asītianubyañjanavicittalakkhaṇasarīro
  aggopi ukkantakārasadisasannikāso suññatānimittāppaṇihitanirodha-
  sayanamāsayo suññavivittakānanavane appasaddaniraghosapaṭisallīno
  attano buddhāsayā abhinikkhamitvā ñāṇavijimhitaṃ vijimhitvā
  sadevakaṃ lokaṃ paloketvā omilitvā avīciṃ nimmilitvā bhavaggaṃ
  etthantare ye keci sattā paripakkamānasā visuddhacittā te
  sabbe iminā nādena anubujjhissanti ye pana kudiṭṭhidassanagatā
  sassatucchedaṃ taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīraṃ taṃ vādaṃ
  ovadantā akatajhānabhāvaṃ aparisakāravādake sabbe iminā nādena
  sakadassanaṃ jahissantīti buddhanādaṃ nadati nadamānassa bhagavato
  buddhanādaṃ bujjhanti bodhaneyyena dissanti viparitadassanā kudiṭṭhivimanā
  honti antakabalalābhattagarusakkāravedhitā. Iminā ca pana cha gaṇino
  pūraṇakassapo makkhaligosālo ajito kesakambalo pakuddho kaccāyano
  sañjayo velaṭṭhaputto nigaṇṭho nāṭaputto ye ca panaññe pañca
  gaṇino te sabbe dhanudiṭṭhā vānarā viya vithitavimanadumanādhomukhā
-------------------------------------------------
[page 446]
  Atuṭṭhā pajjhāyanti ālulikabhūtā padhupayanti tahiṃ tahiṃ padhāvitvā
  nilīnā paṭivasanti. Ye pana bhagavato sāvakā te ca pahaṃsitapahaṃsitā
  pamuditamanā tuṭṭhā sumanā somanassabhūtā vimuttivarakusumapupphitā
  buddhavaranāditāgajjitaṃ suṇitvā. Iminā ca mahārāja hetunā iminā
  kāraṇena iminā nayena iminā anumānena ñātabbaṃ tassa bhagavato
  anantaguṇapāramigato so bhagavāti. Bhavatiha
         anumānena jānanti bhīte disvāna kuppite
         migarājassa saddena bhītāme migapakkhiyo
         tatheva titthiye disvā vitthate bhītamānase
         anumānena ñātabbaṃ dhammarājena gajjitanti.
      Sugajjitaṃ bhante nāgasena bhagavatā sunāditaṃ surasitaṃ sukathitaṃ.
  Tadā bhagavato nādaṃ sutvā ajjāpi aññatitthiyā na saṃvattanti
  bhiyyo buddhabalaṃ dīpehi hetunā kāraṇenāti. Yathā mahārāja
  gaṅgā gambhīratā himavatā savanti mahāsamuddaṃ osaranti yathā
  pana uparipabbate mahāmegho phaṇi vā paṭigajjitavuṭṭho hoti.
  Athāyaṃ gaṅgā gambhīratā samatittikā samapuṇṇā atiriyamānā
  pañca mahānadiyo himavantato pabhavanti pabbatakandaraṃ padaraṃ
  sobbhaṃ paripūramānā kumbhilabhāhāāvattagūsayāni bhañjamānā
  anukulāpādapalatāni samūlamuppādayantā nalaveḷugumbaviraṇagacchāni
  samūlamatthayantā vicchikasatapadīmaṇḍakāhīnaṃ kulāni samuddhayantā nakulakālamiga-
  kukkurasasakaṇaviḷārakoṭṭhakaeṇeyyavarāharohitacappakapasatabheraṇḍakamahisagaṇāni
-------------------------------------------------
[page 447]
  Saṃvattayantā bhesmārūpāni ca kuṇapāni uddhumātapabhūtāni
  kimikulāsayamākiṇṇā upalavantā asuciduggandhajegucchasaṃkārakaṭṭhānāni
  vidhamayantā pāpakaṃ sodhayitvā amitajalamahāsamuddasāgaramahodadhimosaranti.
  Atha pana aparena samayena āpodhātūni saṃvattanti
  tamenaṃ yebhuyyena aññe bhūtā sañcarajanā sakalatanukaddamamakkhitā
  sambhaggamathitapādapagaṇāni nalaveḷugumbalatāni rukkhaggamanugatā
  rurūsubhaeṇeyyavarāhakarūpāni mahoragameṇḍakanuddhamātanalamakkhikasamākiṇṇā
  athassa byāsatto mukhavivaradanto dumattariupantaragatāni ajatarāni
  ca bhesmārūpāni rukkhaggamanugatā chinnalatākoṭi viya
  abhisambandhapādapagaṇe ca samūlamatthitamatthite disvā anumānena jānanti mahā
  vata bho udakarāsī anuvatto bhavissati yathā imāni bhūsāni
  rukkhaggamanugatānīti. Evameva kho mahārāja ayaṃ dhammanadī dhammarājato
  pabhāvitā anupubbena sandamānā ajātājarāmaraṇasukhisitabhāvaṃ asaṃkhataṃ
  nibbānaṃ sāgaravaraṃ osarati. Yathā pana bhagavā mahākaruṇāsamāpattiyā
  vuṭṭhahitvā bodhaneyyamanubodhayanto sadevakaṃ lokaṃ abhitappayanto
  mahatā dhammameghena abhivassati ativuṭṭhe pana bhagavatā
  dhammameghe ayaṃ dhammanadī samatittikā kappeyyā atiriyamānā
  sadevakaṃ lokaṃ abhimottharanto odhunanto rāgarajadosarajamohamānamakkhe
  byāhananto nandiyalatāsakkāyadiṭṭhiṃ vidhamayanto kuvitakkapādapagaṇeva
  samūlamatthayanto kilesamuddhaccavicikicchātaṇhājaṭājaṭitāni
  samūlamatthayanto bhiṃsarūpāni ca pāpakammamesanāni pavāhayanto
-------------------------------------------------
[page 448]
  Rāgapaṅkadosagumbamohakalalamānakaddamaṃ pavāhayanto
  lābhasakkārabhūsapaṅkamupilaṃ pavāhayanto subhanimittapādapāni matthayanto
  līnaviriyakosajjakalahavivādapāpakeva saṃvattayanto kodhupanāhamāyāmakkheva
  upasamento sabbadosāni vinivattayanto abhayaparipūramahādhammaparamāmatanibbāna-
  sāgaravaraṃ osaritvā arahattehi cittaṃ vimoceti. Dhammasāgaro
  kho mahārāja bhagavato antopāramitadhammavīciyo na tassa onattaṃ
  vā puṇṇattaṃ vā paññāyati. Iminā kāraṇena iminā hetunā
  iminā nayena iminā anumānena ñātabbaṃ appameyyo so bhagavā
  sabbaguṇehīti. Bhavatiha
         laggaṃ disvā bhūsapaṅkaṃ kalalaṃ mandagataṃ mahiṃ
         anumānena jānanti vārikkhandho mahāgato
         tathevimaṃ janaṃ disvā rajapaṅkadosamohakaṃ
         vahitaṃ dhammanadiyā vissaṭṭhaṃ dhammasāgare
         dhammāmatagataṃ disvā sadevakaṃ imaṃ mahiṃ
         anumānena ñātabbaṃ dhammakkhandho mahāgatoti.
      Bhante nāgasena kilesapavāhanatthāya bhagavatā dhammanadī
  paññattāti. Āma mahārājāti. Tenahi bhiyyo buddhabalaṃ dīpehi
  hetunā kāraṇenāti. Yathā mahārāja paṭivāte nāgasilapunnāgacampaka-
  adhimuttakamadhugandhikadhanukārikātālīsamupādāya rammakakālasamaye
  pupphitā dibbagandhā supavāyanti adhipavāyanti tamenaṃ anuvātamanugatā
  janā pupphagandhamanughāyantā anumānena jānanti paṭivātaṃ kho
-------------------------------------------------
[page 449]
  Nānādumalatā pupphitā bhavissanti yathāyaṃ anuvāte dibbadibbayagandhā
  supavāyanti. Evameva kho mahārāja ayaṃ sadevako loko
  sīlagandhena chupito kugaṇiparappavādāni abhibhuyya sīlapavaragandho pavāyati.
  Katamāni tāni varagandhasīlāni. Tīṇi ca saraṇagamanāni pañca
  pāṭimokkhapariyāpannāni ca sīlāni uddesapariyāpannāni ca sīlāni
  cārittavāritāni ca sīlāni. Imāni kho mahārāja sīlāni yehi sīlehi
  sadevako loko dibbadibbayagandho sampavāyati. Iminā mahārāja
  kāraṇena iminā hetunā iminā nayena iminā anumānena ñātabbaṃ
  atthi so bhagavāti. Ayaṃ sadevako loko sīlagandhena suchupito. Bhavatiha
         anumānena jānanti ghāyitvā gandhuttamaṃ
         pavāyati yathā gandho hessanti pupphitā dumā
         tathevidaṃ sīlagandhaṃ pavāyati sadevake
         anumānena ñātabbaṃ atthi buddho anuttaroti.
      Evarūpena kho mahārāja kāraṇasahassena hetusahassena
  nayasahassena na sakkā upadassayituṃ buddhabalaṃ paridīpayituṃ. Yathā
  mahārāja nānāpuppharāsī bhaveyya tamenaṃ kusalo mālākāro
  mālākārantevāsī vā yo jano kusalo ācariyamanusatthiyā
  paccattapurisakārena vicittaṃ mālaṃ gantheyya vijjamānesu pupphesu evameva kho
  mahārāja so bhagavā vicittapuppharāsimiva anekavaṇṇānekasatasahassa-
  vaṇṇāsaṃkheyyo appameyyo ahametarahi jinasāsanamālakāro viya
-------------------------------------------------
[page 450]
  Pubbakānaṃ ācariyānaṃ vasenapi mayhaṃ ganthamatibuddhiyā asaṃkheyyenapi
  kāraṇena anumānena buddhabalaṃ dīpayissāmi tathevamettha tvaṃ mahārāja
  chandaṃ janehīti.
      Dukkaraṃ bhante nāgasena aññesaṃ evarūpena anumānena paṭirūpakena
  buddhabalaṃ upadassayituṃ attanopi tattha paripuṇṇasaṅkappo tava buddhabalaṃ
  paridīpena. Nibbutosmi bhante nāgasena tumhākaṃ paramavicittena
  pañhābyākaraṇenāti.
                    Anumānapañho aṭṭhamo.
         Passatāraññike bhikkhū ajjhogāḷhe dhutaṅguṇe
         puna passati gihī rājā anāgāmiphale ṭhite
         ubhopi te cintayitvā uppajji saṃsayo mahā
         yadi bujjhati gihī dhamme niratthaṃ hoti dhutaṅguṇaṃ
         paravādivādamathanaṃ nipuṇaṃ sāsanaṃ dharaṃ
         handa pucchāmi kathiseṭṭhaṃ so me kaṅkhaṃ vinessatīti.
      Atha kho milindo rājā haṭṭho tuṭṭho pamudito udaggo
  ānandito sumano vedajāto āyūhito uyyāto chandajāto pītito
  paṭipanno okkanto vimatipathaṃ tāritukāmo goṇo viya pipāsito
  udakagāmī chāto viya puriso bhojanaṃ gavesī paṇṭhakova satthaṃ
  paṭimānī āturo viya bhiṃsakaṃ pariyesī adhano viya nidhiṃ gavesī ca
  taraṇo viya nāvāpaṭikaṅkhiko kāmuko viya piyasamāgamicchi vādito
  viya visaddakāmo bhīto viya saraṇaṃ gavesī jinaputto viya
-------------------------------------------------
[page 451]
  Vimuttikāmo turitaturito theraṃ nāgasenamupasaṅkami. Bhavatiha
         pipāsito yathā goṇo chātova bhojanatthiko
         gāmikova satthapaṭimānī āturova tikicchakaṃ
         adhanova nidhiṃ gavesi nāvaṃva pāragāmiko
         kāmukova piyamicchi vādiko visaddamesi
         bhītova saraṇaṃ gavesi jinaputto viya nibbutanti.
      Evaṃ milindo rājā vimatitaritukāmo theraṃ nāgasenaṃ upasaṅkami
  upasaṅkamitvā ca pana milindo rājā kavinā saddhiṃ sārāṇīyaṃ
  anupavedetvā dasavidhaṃ guṇavaraṃ samanupassitvā pucchati nissaṃsayo
  bhavissāmi vimatipathaṃ vītivatto visuddhaṃ me mānasaṃ bhavissati
  anovaddhabyāpagato kuvitakko bhavissāmi dhammasotaṃ samāpanno
  paññācakkhuṃ paṭilabhissāmi ācariyamanupaviṭṭho okampito bhavissāmi patthito
  sabbakusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ nirāvaraṇo bhavissāmi lokuttarehi dhammehi
  anussato bhavissāmi sabbabhavehi abhīto bhavissāmi parisantaraṃ gato
  khippaṃ dassanaṃ bhavissāmi dhammaṃ paṭivedhitunti. Ime dasavidhe
  guṇavare samanupassitvā pucchati
      bhante nāgasena atthi koci gihībhūto agāriko kāmabhogī
  puttadārasambādhasayanaṃ ajjhāvasanto kāsikacandanaṃ paccanubhonto
  mālāgandhavilepanaṃ dhārayanto jātarūparajaṭaṃ sādiyanto maṇimuttākāñcanamoliṃ
  bandhanto yo santaṃ paramatthaṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikarotīti. Natthi mahārāja
  ekaññeva sataṃ na dve satāni na ca sahassāni na ca
-------------------------------------------------
[page 452]
  Satasahassāni na koṭisatāni na koṭisahassāni na koṭisatasahassāni. Tiṭṭhatu
  mahārāja dasannaṃ vīsatiyā satassa abhisamayo katamena te pariyāyena
  anuyogaṃ dammīti. Tvamevetaṃ patthitaṃ brūhīti. Tenahi mahārāja
  te kathayissāmi satena vā sahassena vā satasahassena vā koṭiyā
  vā koṭisatena vā koṭisahassena vā. Yā kāci navaṅge
  buddhasāsane sallekhitācārapaṭipattidhutaguṇavaraṅganissitā kathā tā sabbā idha
  samosaranti. Yathā nāma mahārāja ninnunnatasamavisamathalajaladesabhāge
  abhivuṭṭhaṃ udakaṃ taṃ sabbaṃ tato nivattitvā mahodadhiṃ sāgaraṃ osari
  evameva kho mahārāja sampādake sati yā kāci navaṅgabuddhavacane
  sallekhitācārapaṭipattidhutaguṇavaraṅganissitā kathā tā sabbā idha
  samosaranti. Mayhamettha mahārāja paribyattatāya vuddhiyā kāraṇaparidīpanaṃ
  samosarissati teneso attho suvibhatto suvicitto paripuṇṇo
  pūritasamatitto bhavissati. Yathā mahārāja kusalo lekhācariyo
  anusiṭṭho lekhaṃ osārāpento attano paribyattatāya vuddhiyā
  kāraṇaparidīpanena lekhaṃ paripūreti evameva mayhamettha paribyattatāya
  vuddhiyā kāraṇaparidīpanaṃ samosarissati teneso attho suvibhatto
  suvicitto paripuṇṇo pūritasamatitto bhavissati.
      Nagare mahārāja sāvatthiyaṃ pañcakoṭimattā ariyasāvakā
  bhagavato upāsakaupāsikāyo sattapaññāsa sahassāni tīṇi ca
  satasahassāni anāgāmiphale patiṭṭhitāni tepi sabbe gihībhūtā na
  pabbajitā. Puna ca tattheva gaṇḍāmbamūle vīsati pāṇakoṭiyo.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 453]
  Puna ca rāhulovāde mahāmaṅgalasuttante samacittapariyāye
  parābhavasuttante cūḷasubhaddāsuttante kalahavivādasuttante cūḷabyūhasuttante
  mahābyūhasuttante tuvaṭakasuttante sārīputtasuttante gaṇanapathamatītānaṃ
  devatānaṃ dhammābhisamayo ahosi. Nagare rājagahe paññāsa
  sahassāni tīṇi satasahassāni ariyasāvakā bhagavato upāsakaupāsikāyo
  puna tattheva dhanapālahatthināgadamane navuti pāṇakoṭiyo.
  Pārāyanasamāgame pāsāṇake cetiye cuddasa pāṇakoṭiyo. Puna
  indasālaguhāyaṃ asīti devatākoṭiyo. Puna bārāṇasiyaṃ isipatane
  migadāye paṭhame dhammadesane aṭṭhārasa brahmakoṭiyo aparimāṇā ca
  devatāyo. Puna tāvatiṃsabhavane paṇḍukambalasilāyaṃ abhidhammadesanāya
  asīti devatākoṭiyo. Devorohane saṅkassanagaradvāre
  lokavivaraṇapāṭihāriye pasannānaṃ naramarūnaṃ tiṃsa koṭiyo abhisamiṃsu.
  Puna sakkesu kapilavatthusamiṃ nigrodhārāme buddhavaṃsadesanāya
  mahāsamayasuttantadesanāya ca gaṇanapathamatītānaṃ devatānaṃ dhammābhisamayo
  ahosi. Puna sumanamālākārasamāgame garahadinnasamāgame
  ānandaseṭṭhisamāgame jambukājīvakasamāgame maṇḍūkadevaputtasamāgame
  maṭṭhakuṇḍalidevaputtasamāgame sulasānagarasobhinisamāgame sirimānagarasobhini-
  samāgame pesakāradhītusamāgame cūḷasubhaddāsamāgame sāketabrāhmaṇassa
  āḷāhanadassanasamāgame sunāparantakasamāgame sakkapañhasamāgame
  tirokuḍḍasamāgame ratanasuttasamāgame paccekaṃ caturāsītiyā pāṇasahassānaṃ
  dhammābhisamayo ahosi. Yāvatā mahārāja bhagavā loke aṭṭhāsi
-------------------------------------------------
[page 454]
  Soḷasasu mahājanapadesu yattha yattha bhagavā vihāsi tattha tattha
  yebhuyyena dve tayo cattāro pañca dasa devamanussā sataṃ ca
  sahassaṃ ca satasahassaṃ ca te sabbe santaṃ paramatthaṃ nibbānaṃ
  sacchikariṃsu. Ye te mahārāja devā gihīyeva te na pabbajitā.
  Etāni ca mahārāja aññāni ca anekāni devatākoṭisatasahassāni
  gihī agārikā kāmabhogino santaṃ paramatthaṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikariṃsūti.
      Yadi bhante nāgasena gihī agārikā kāmabhogino santaṃ
  paramatthaṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikaronti atha imāni terasa dhutaṅgaguṇāni
  kimatthaṃ sādhenti tena kāraṇena dhutaṅgaguṇāni akiccakarāni honti.
  Yadi bhante nāgasena mantaparijappanena byādhiyo upasamenti kiṃ pana
  virecanena mantamanussena. Yadi muṭṭhīhi paṭisattuṃ niggaho bhavati
  kiṃ asisattisaracāpakodaṇḍadabbidaṇḍalaguḷamuggarehi. Yadi
  gaṇṭhikuṭilasusirakaṭṭhasākhabandhanena rukkhamabhirūhanaṃ bhavati kiṃ dīghadaḷhasubaddha-
  nisseṇipariyesanena. Yadi thandilaseyyāya dhātusamatā bhavati kiṃ
  sukhumasamphassamahatimahāsirisayanapariyesanena. Yadi eko sāsaṅkasabhaya-
  visamakantāratāraṇasamattho bhavati kiṃ sannaddhasajjanasahāyamahāsattha-
  pariyesanena. Yadi nadiṃ sakabāhāya tarituṃ samattho bhavati kiṃ pana
  dhuvasetu dhuvanāvāpariyesanena. Yadi sakasantakena ghāsacchādo bhavati
  kiṃ parasevanāpiyasamullāpapacchāpuredhāvanena. Yadi akhātataḷāke
  udakaṃ labhati kiṃ udapānataḷākapokkharaṇikhaṇanena. Evameva kho
  bhante nāgasena yadi gihī agārikā kāmabhogī santaṃ paramatthaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 455]
  Nibbānaṃ sacchikaronti kiṃ dhutaṅgaguṇavarasamādiyanenāti.
      Aṭṭhavīsati kho panime mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇā yathābhuccadhutaṅgaguṇā
  yehi guṇehi dhutaṅgaṃ sabbabuddhānaṃ pihitaṃ patthitaṃ. Katame
  aṭṭhavīsati. Idha mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ suddhājīvaṃ sukhaphalaṃ anavajjaṃ
  parassa dukkhāpanudaṃ abhayaṃ asampīḷaṃ ekantavuddhikaṃ aparihāniyaṃ amāyaṃ
  anāvilaṃ ārakkhaṃ patthitadadaṃ sabbasattadhupanaṃ saṃvarahitaṃ paṭirūpaṃ
  atisantaṃ vippamuttaṃ rāgakkhayaṃ dosavūpamasanaṃ mohavināsanaṃ
  mānapavāhanaṃ kuvitakkachejjakāraṇaṃ kaṅkhāvitaraṇaṃ kosajjaviddhaṃsanaṃ
  aratipavāhanaṃ khamanaṃ atulaṃ appamāṇaṃ sabbadukkhakkhayagāminanti. Ime kho
  mahārāja aṭṭhavīsati dhutaṅgaguṇā yathābhuccadhutaṅgaguṇā yehi guṇehi
  dhutaṅgaṃ sabbabuddhānaṃ pihitaṃ patthitaṃ.
      Ye kho te mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇe samādāya sevanti te
  aṭṭhārasahaṅgehi samupetā bhavanti. Katamehi aṭṭhārasahi. Ācāro
  tesaṃ suvisuddho hoti paṭipadā supūritā hoti kāyikavācasikaṃ surakkhitaṃ
  hoti mano suvisuddho hoti viriyaṃ supaggahitaṃ hoti bhayaṃ vūpasamati
  attānudiṭṭhi byapagatā hoti āghāto vūpasanto hoti mettā
  upaṭṭhitā hoti āhāro pariññāto hoti sabbasattānaṃ garukato
  hoti bhojane mattaññū hoti jāgariyamanuyutto hoti aniketo hoti
  yattha phāsu tattha vihārī hoti pāpajegucchī hoti vivekārāmo hoti
  satataṃ appamatto hoti. Ye te mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇe samādāya
  sevanti te imehi aṭṭhārasahi aṅgehi samupetā bhavanti.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 456]
      Dasa ime mahārāja puggalā dhutaṅgaguṇārahā. Katame
  dasa. Saddho hirimā dhitimā akuho atthavasī alolo sikkhākāmo
  daḷhasamādāno anujjhānabahulo mettāvihārī. Ime kho mahārāja
  dasa puggalā dhutaṅgaguṇārahā.
      Ye te mahārāja gihī agārikā kāmabhogino .pe. Santaṃ
  paramatthaṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikaronti te sabbe purimāsu jātīsu terasasu
  dhutaṅgaguṇesu katupāsanā katabhūmikammā te tatthevācārañca
  paṭipattiñca sodhayitvā ajjetarahi gihīyeva santā santaṃ paramatthaṃ
  nibbānaṃ sacchikaronti. Yathā mahārāja kusalo issattho sisso
  paṭhamaṃ tāva upāsanasālāyaṃ cāpabhedacāpāropanagahaṇamuṭṭhi-
  paṭipīḷanāṅgulināmapādaṭhapanasaragahaṇasandahanāakaḍḍhanasanthāraṇalakkhaniyamanakhipane
  tiṇapurisakachaṇakatiṇapalālamattikāpuñjaphalakalakkhavedhe anusikkhitvā
  rañño santike upāsanaṃ dassetvā rājānaṃ ārādhayitvā
  ājaññarathagajaturaṅgadhanadhaññahiraññasuvaṇṇadāsidāsabhariyāgāmanigamavaraṃ
  labhati evameva kho mahārāja ye te gihī agārikā kāmabhogī
  .pe. Santaṃ paramatthaṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikaronti te sabbe purimāsu
  jātīsu terasadhutaṅgaguṇesu katupāsanā katabhūmikammā te tatthevācārañca
  pāṭipattiñca sodhayitvā ajjetarahi gihīyeva santā santaṃ
  paramatthaṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikaronti. Na mahārāja pubbe dhutaṅgaguṇāsevanena
  vinā tāyeva jātiyā arahattaṃ sacchikiriyā hoti.
  Uttamena viriyena uttamāya paṭipattiyā tathārūpena ācārena
-------------------------------------------------
[page 457]
  Sotāpattiphalaṃ sacchikiriyā hoti. Yathā vā pana mahārāja bhiṃsako
  sallakanto pubbe ācariyaṃ dhanena vā vattapaṭipattiyā vā
  ārādhayitvā satthaggahaṇavasena lekhanachedanasalluddharaṇavaṇadhovana-
  sosanabhesajjānulepanavimanavirecanānuvāsanasaṃyojanakiriyaṃ anusikkhitvā vijjāsu
  katasikkho katupāsano sikkhitahattho kadāci āture upasaṅkamati
  tikicchāya evameva kho mahārāja gihī agārikā kāmabhogino santaṃ
  paramatthaṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikaronti te sabbe purimāsu jātīsu
  terasadhutaṅgaguṇesu katupāsanā katabhūmikammā te tatthevācārañca
  paṭipattiñca sodhayitvā ajjetarahi gihīyeva santā santaṃ paramatthaṃ
  nibbānaṃ sacchikaronti. Na mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇena avisuddhānaṃ
  dhammābhisamayo hoti. Yathā mahārāja udakassa asecanena vījānaṃ
  anāruho bhavati evameva kho mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇena avisuddhānaṃ
  dhammābhisamayo na hoti. Yathā vā pana mahārāja akatakusalānaṃ
  sugatigamanaṃ na hoti evameva kho mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇena avisuddhānaṃ
  dhammābhisamayo na hoti.
      Paṭhavīsamaṃ mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ patiṭṭhānatthena.
  Āposamaṃ mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ sabbakilesamaladhovanatthena.
  Tejosamaṃ mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ sabbakilesavanajjhāpanatthena.
  Vāyosamaṃ mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ
  sabbakilesarajopavāhanatthena. Agadasamaṃ mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 458]
  Visuddhikāmānaṃ sabbakilesabyādhivūpasamanatthena. Amatasamaṃ mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ
  visuddhikāmānaṃ sabbakilesavisanāsanatthena. Khettasamaṃ mahārāja
  dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ sabbasāmaññaguṇasassavirūhanatthena. Manoharasamaṃ
  mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ sabbapatthitasampattidadatthena.
