  Nāvāsamaṃ mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ aṇṇavataraṇasaṃsārapāragamanatthena.
  Bhīrutāṇasamaṃ mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ
  jarāmaraṇabhayabhītānaṃ assāsakaraṇatthena. Mātusamaṃ mahārāja
  dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ kilesadukkhapaṭipīḷitānaṃ anuggahakatthena. Pitusamaṃ
  mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ kusalavuḍḍhikāmānaṃ
  sabbasāmaññajanakatthena. Mittasamaṃ mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ
  sabbasāmaññapariyesanāvisaṃvādakatthena. Padumasamaṃ mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ
  visuddhikāmānaṃ sabbakilesehi anūpalittatthena. Catujātivaragandhasamaṃ
  mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ kilesaduggandhapaṭivinodanatthena.
  Girirājavarasamaṃ mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ aṭṭhalokadhammehi
  akampiyatthena. Ākāsasamaṃ mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ
  sabbatthagahaṇāpagatauruvisaṭavitthatamahantatthena. Najjosamaṃ mahārāja
  dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ kilesamalapavāhanatthena. Desakasamaṃ mahārāja
  dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ jātikantārakilesadiṭṭhi visamābhinīharaṇatthena.
  Mahāsatthavāhasamaṃ mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ sabbabhayasuñña-
  khemābhayapavarapuranibbānanagarasampāpanatthena. Vimalavaraparisuddhi-
  saniddhasumajjitāadāsasamaṃ mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ saṃkhārānaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 459]
  Sabhāvadassanatthena. Phalakasamaṃ mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ
  kilesalaguḷasarasattipaṭibāhanatthena. Candasamaṃ mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ
  visuddhikāmānaṃ ariyānaṃ icchitapatthitamanāpatthena. Suriyasamaṃ mahārāja
  dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ visuddhikāmānaṃ sabbatamatimiraviddhaṃsanatthena.
      Evaṃ kho mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ bahūpakāraṃ hitakaraṃ phāsukaraṃ
  piyakaraṃ avajjakaraṃ vahikaraṃ patiṭṭhitaṃ yasāvahaṃ sukhāhāraṃ sukhavipākaṃ
  guṇarāsi guṇapuñjaṃ aparimitavaraguṇaṃ sabbattha pavaraggaṭṭhānaṃ bhayanudaṃ
  sokanudaṃ dukkhanudaṃ darathanudaṃ pariḷāhanudaṃ aratinudaṃ bhavanudaṃ khilanudaṃ
  rāganudaṃ dosanudaṃ mohanudaṃ diṭṭhinudaṃ sabbākusaladhammanudaṃ.
      Yathā mahārāja manujā upatthambhavasena bhojanaṃ upasevanti
  hitavasena bhesajjaṃ upasevanti upakāravasena mittaṃ upasevanti
  otāraṇavasena nāvaṃ upasevanti sugandhavasena mālāgandhaṃ upasevanti
  abhayavasena bhīrutāṇaṃ upasevanti patiṭṭhānavasena paṭhaviṃ upasevanti
  sippavasena ācāriyaṃ upasevanti yasavasena rājānaṃ upasevanti
  kāmadadavasena maṇiratanaṃ upasevanti evameva kho mahārāja
  sabbasāmaññadadavasena ariyā dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ upasevanti.
      Yathā vā pana mahārāja udakaṃ bījavirūhanāya aggi jhāpanāya
  āhāro balāharaṇāya latā bandhanāya satthaṃ chedanāya pānīyaṃ
  pipāsavinayāya nidhi assāsakaraṇāya nāvā taraṇāya bhesajjaṃ
  byādhivūpasamāya yānaṃ sukhagamanāya bhīrutāṇaṃ bhayavinodanāya rājā
  ārakkhakatthāya phalakaṃ daṇḍaleḍḍulaguḷasarasattipaṭibāhanāya ācariyo
-------------------------------------------------
[page 460]
  Anusāsanāya mātā posanāya ādāso ālokanāya alaṅkāro sobhanāya
  vatthaṃ paṭicchādanāya nisseṇi ārohanāya tulā nikkhipanāya mantaṃ
  jappanāya āvudhaṃ tajjaniyapaṭibāhanāya padīpo andhakārahanāya vāto
  pariḷāhananibbāpanāya sippaṃ vuttinibbattanāya agadaṃ jīvitarakkhanāya
  ākaro ratanuppādāya ratanaṃ alaṅkārāya āṇā anatikkamāya
  issariyaṃ vasavattanāya evameva kho mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ sāmaññabījavirūhanāya
  kilesamalajhāpanāya iddhibalāharaṇāya satisaṃvaranibandhanāya
  vimativicikicchāsallavinodanāya taṇhāpipāsavinayāya abhisamayāssāsakaraṇāya
  caturoghataraṇāya kilesabyādhivūpasamanāya nibbānasukhalahugamanāya
  jātijarābyādhimaraṇasokaparidevadukkhadomanassaupāyāsasabbabhayasantāsa-
  vūpasamanāya sāmaññaguṇaparirakkhanāya kuvitakkakilesapaṭibāhanāya
  kevalasakalasāmaññamanusāsanāya sabbasāmaññavaraposanāya samathavipassanā-
  maggaphalanibbānadassanāya sakalalokathūtathomitamahatisobhaguṇakaraṇāya
  sabbāpāyapidahanāya sāmaññatthaselasikharamuddhaniabhirūhanāya vaṅkakuṭila-
  visamacittapaṭivattanikkhipanāya sevitabbāsevitabbadhammasajjhāyakaraṇāya
  sabbakilesasattutajjanāya mahāavijjandhakāraghātanāya tividhaggisantāpa-
  pariḷāhanibbāpanāya santasukhumasamāpattisādhanāya catusaccābhisamaya-
  nibbattanāya sīlasamantakevalasakalasāmaññaparirakkhaṇāya chaḷabhiññā-
  vararatanauppādāya yogijanālaṅkaraṇāya anavajjanipuṇasukhumapaṇītasantisukhaṃ
  anatikkamāya kevalasakalasāmaññāriyadhammavasavattanāya. Itipi
  mahārāja imesaṃ guṇānaṃ adhigamāya yadidaṃ ekamekaṃ dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ. Evaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 461]
  Mahārāja atulaṃ dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ appameyyaṃ asamaṃ appaṭisamaṃ appaṭibhāgaṃ
  uttamaṃ anuttaraṃ seṭṭhaṃ visiṭṭhaṃ adhikaṃ āyataṃ puthulaṃ visaṭaṃ vitthataṃ
  visālaṃ vitthinnaṃ garukaṃ mahantaṃ.
      Yo kho mahārāja puggalo pāpiccho icchāpakato kuhako
  luddho odariko lābhakāmo yasakāmo kittikāmo ayutto appatto
  ananucchaviko anaraho appaṭirūpo dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ samādiyati. So diguṇaṃ
  daṇḍamāpajjati sabbaguṇaghātamāpajjati diṭṭhadhammikañca
  samparāyikañca. Diṭṭhadhammikaṃpi hīḷanaṃ khīlanaṃ garahanaṃ upphaṇḍanaṃ khipanaṃ
  asambhogaṃ nissāraṇaṃ nicchubhaṇaṃ pavāhanaṃ pavajjanaṃ paṭilabhati.
  Samparāyepi satayojanike avīcimahāniraye uṇhakaṭhitatattasantatta-
  accijālāmālake anekavassasatasahassāni uddhamadho tiriyaṃ pheṇuddehakaṃ
  samparivattakaṃ paccati tato muccatvā mahantehi kāyehi bhīmarūpehi
  asuddhapanasūsirarūpehi sūnuddhumātasusiruttamaṅgo chāto pipāsito pacchato
  ca purato ca asampayogarūpavaṇṇo bhaggakaṇṇasoto
  ummilitanimmilitanettanayano arugattapakkagatto puḷavākiṇṇasabbakāyo
  matasunakhapaṭikkulo vātamukhe viya aggikkhandho anto jalamāno pajjalamāno
  aleṇo asaraṇo kāruññaṃ paridevamāno mahāpeto nijjhāmataṇhiko
  hutvā āhiṇḍamāno mahiyā aṭṭasaraṃ karoti. Yathā
  mahārāja yo koci jano ayutto appatto ananucchaviko anaraho
  appaṭirūpo hīno kujātiko khattiyābhisekena abhisiñcati so labhati
  hatthacchedaṃ pādacchedaṃ hatthapādacchedaṃ kaṇṇacchedaṃ nāsacchedaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 462]
  Kaṇṇanāsacchedaṃ vilaṅgathālikaṃ saṅkhamuṇḍikaṃ rāhumukhaṃ jotimālikaṃ
  hatthappajjotikaṃ erakavattikaṃ cīrakavāsikaṃ eṇeyyakaṃ baḷisamaṃsikaṃ
  kahāpaṇakaṃ khārāpaṭicchakaṃ palighaparivattakaṃ palālapīṭhakaṃ tattena telena
  osiñcanaṃ sunakhakhādāpanaṃ jīvasūlāropanaṃ asinā sīsacchedaṃ anekavihitaṃ
  kammakaraṇaṃ kaṭukaṃ anubhavati. Kiṃ tattha kāraṇaṃ. Ayutto
  appatto ananucchaviko anaraho appaṭirūpo hīno kujātiko mahante
  issariyaṭṭhāne attānaṃ ṭhapesi kevalaṃ ghātesi. Evameva kho
  mahārāja yo koci pāpiccho .pe. Mahiyā aṭṭasaraṃ karoti.
      Yo pana mahārāja yutto patto anucchaviko paṭirūpo
  araho appiccho santuṭṭho vivitto asaṃsaṭṭho āraddhaviriyo
  pahitatto asaṭho amāyo na odariko na lābhakāmo na yasakāmo
  na kittikāmo saddho saddhāya pabbajito jarāmaraṇā vimuccitukāmo
  jinasāsanaṃ paggaṇhāmīti dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ samādiyati so diguṇaṃ pūjaṃ
  arahati devānañca manussānañca piyo hoti manāpo pihito
  patthito jātisumanamallikādipupphaṃ viya nahātānulittassa jighacchitassa
  viya paṇītabhojanaṃ pipāsitassa viya sītalavimalamadhurapānīyaṃ visagatassa
  viya osathavaraṃ sīghagamanakāmassa viya ājaññarathavaruttamaṃ
  pattikāmassa viya sīghayānaṃ bahuvividhātthakāmassa viya manoharamaṇiratanaṃ
  keḷitukāmassa viya pañcāṅgapavaraturiyaṃ abhiramitukāmassa viya nāṭakagaṇaṃ
  abhisiñcitukāmassa viya paṇḍaravimalapavarasetacchattaṃ dhammakāmassa viya
  arahattaphalādhigamana muttamaṃ. Yaṃ kiñci jinasāsanaṃ aggavaraṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 463]
  Yasasukhasaṃvattanaṃ. Tassa cattāro satipaṭṭhānā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti
  cattāro sammappadhānā cattāro iddhipādā pañcindriyāni pañca
  balāni satta bojjhaṅgā ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo bhāvanāpāripūriṃ
  gacchati samathavipassanā adhigacchati paṭivedhaṃ paṭimāneti cattāri
  sāmaññaphalāni catasso paṭisambhidā tisso vijjā chaḷabhiññāyo
  kevalo ca samaṇadhammo sabbe tassādheyyā honti vimuttipaṇḍara-
  vimalasetacchattena abhisiñcati. Yathā mahārāja rañño khattiyassa
  abhijātakulakulinassa khattiyābhisekena abhisittassa paricaranti
  saraṭṭhanegamajānapadabhaṭabalatthā aṭṭhatiṃsā ca rājapurisā naṭanaccakavigaṇa-
  mukhamaṅgalikā sotthivācakā samaṇabrāhmaṇapāsaṇḍagaṇā abhigacchanti
  yaṃ kiñci paṭhaviyā paṭṭanaratanākarataḷākarasuṅkaṭṭhānaverajjakachejja-
  bhejjajanamanusāsanaṃ sabbattha sāmiko bhavati evameva kho mahārāja
  yo koci puggalo yutto patto .pe. Vimuttipaṇḍaravimalasetacchattena
  abhisiñcati.
      Terasimāni mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇāni yehi suddhikato
  mahānibbānasāgaraṃ pavisitvā bahuvidhadhammakīḷamabhikīḷati rūpārūpāṭṭhasamāpattiyo
  valañjeti iddhividhaṃ dibbasotadhātuṃ paracittavijānanaṃ pubbenivāsānussatiṃ
  dibbacakkhuṃ sabbāsavakkhayañca pāpuṇāti. Katamāni
  terasa. Paṃsukūlikaṅgaṃ tecīvarikaṅgaṃ piṇḍapātikaṅgaṃ sapadānacārikaṅgaṃ
  ekāsanikaṅgaṃ pattapiṇḍikaṅgaṃ khalupacchābhattikaṅgaṃ āraññikaṅgaṃ
  rukkhamūlikaṅgaṃ abbhokāsikaṅgaṃ sosānikaṅgaṃ yathāsanthatikaṅgaṃ nesajjikaṅgaṃ.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 464]
  Imehi kho mahārāja terasadhutaṅgaguṇehi pubbe āsevitehi nisevitehi
  ciṇṇehi pariciṇṇehi upadhāritehi paripūritehi kevalaṃ sāmaññaṃ
  paṭilabhati tassādheyyā honti kevalā santā sukhā samāpattiyo.
      Yathā mahārāja sadhano nāviko paṭṭane suṭṭha katasuṅko
  mahāsamuddaṃ pavisitvā vaṅgaṃ takkolaṃ cīnaṃ sovīraṃ suraṭṭhaṃ alasandaṃ
  kolapaṭṭanaṃ suvaṇṇabhūmiṃ gacchati aññaṃpi yaṃ kiñci nāvāsañcaraṇaṃ
  evameva kho mahārāja imehi terasadhutaṅgaguṇehi pubbe āsevitehi
  nisevitehi ciṇṇehi pariciṇṇehi upadhāritehi paripūritehi kevalaṃ
  sāmaññaṃ paṭilabhati tassādheyyā honti kevalā santā sukhā
  samāpattiyo.
      Yathā vā pana mahārāja kasako paṭhamaṃ khettadosaṃ
  tiṇakaṭṭhapāsāṇaṃ apanetvā kasitvā vapitvā sammā udakaṃ pavesetvā
  rakkhitvā gopetvā lavanamaddanena bahudhaññako bhavati tassādheyyā
  honti ye keci avasesā adhanā kapaṇā daliddā duggatajanā
  evameva kho mahārāja imehi terasadhutaṅgaguṇehi pubbe āsevitehi
  nisevitehi .pe. Kevalā santā sukhā samāpattiyo.
      Yathā vā pana mahārāja khattiyo muddhāvasitto abhijātakulakulino
  chejjabhejjajanamanusāsane issaro hoti vasavattiko sāmiko
  icchākaraṇo kevalā ca mahāpaṭhavī tassādheyyā hoti evameva kho
  mahārāja imehi terasadhutaṅgaguṇehi pubbe āsevitehi nisevitehi
  ciṇṇehi pariciṇṇehi upadhāritehi paripūritehi jinasāsanavare issaro
-------------------------------------------------
[page 465]
  Hoti vasavattiko sāmiko icchākaraṇo kevalā ca samaṇaguṇā
  tassādheyyā honti.
      Nanu mahārāja thero upaseno vaṅgantaputto sallekhadhutaṅguṇe
  paripūrikāritāya anādayitvā sāvatthiyaṃ saṃghassa katikaṃ sapariso naravasabhaṃ
  paṭisallānagataṃ upasaṅkamitvā sirasā bhagavantaṃ abhivādetvā ekamantaṃ
  nisīdi. Bhagavā ca taṃ suvinītaṃ parisaṃ oloketvā haṭṭho tuṭṭho
  pamudito parisāya saddhiṃ sallāpaṃ sallapitvā āsabhivacanena idamavoca
  pāsādikā vata tyāyaṃ upasena parisā kathaṃ tvaṃ upasena parisaṃ vinesīti.
  Sopi sabbaññunā dasabalena devātidevena puṭṭho tuṭṭho haṭṭho
  pamudito yathāsabhāvayathābhūtaguṇavasena bhagavantaṃ etadavoca yo koci maṃ
  bhante upasaṅkamitvā pabbajjaṃ vā nissayaṃ vā yācati tamahaṃ evaṃ vadāmi
  ahaṃ kho āvuso āraññiko piṇḍapātiko paṃsukūliko tecīvariko sace
  tvaṃ āraññiko bhavissasi piṇḍapātiko paṃsukūliko tecīvariko evāhaṃ
  taṃ pabbājessāmi nissayaṃ dassāmīti sace so me bhante paṭisuṇitvā
  nandati na oramati evāhaṃ taṃ pabbājemi nissayaṃ demi no ce
  nandati oramati na taṃ pabbājemi na nissayaṃ dammi evāhaṃ bhante parisaṃ
  vinemīti. Evaṃpi mahārāja dhutaṅgaguṇavarasamādinno bhikkhu jinasāsane
  issaro hoti vasavattiko sāmiko icchākaraṇo tassādheyyā honti
  kevalā santā sukhā samāpattiyo.
      Yathā mahārāja padumaṃ nāma abhivutthaṃ pubbe parisuddhaudiccajāti-
  vījappabhavatāya dasaguṇasamupetaṃ. Katame dasa. Siniddhaṃ mudu subhaniyaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 466]
  Sugandhapiyaṃ patthitaṃ pasaṭṭhaṃ jalakaddamānupalittaṃ anupattakesarakaṇṇikāhi
  maṇḍitaṃ bhamaragaṇasevitaṃ sītalasalilasaṃvaddhaṃ. Idaṃ dasaguṇasamupetaṃ
  padumaṃ. Evameva kho mahārāja imehi terasadhutaṅgaguṇehi pubbe
  āsevitehi nisevitehi ciṇṇehi pariciṇṇehi upadhāritehi paripūritehi
  ariyasāvako tiṃsaguṇavarehi samupeto bhavati. Katamehi tiṃsaguṇavarehi.
  Siniddhamudumaddavamettacitto hoti. Ghātitahatavigatakileso hoti.
  Hatanihatamānadiṭṭho hoti. Acaladaḷhaniviṭṭhanibbematikasaddho hoti.
  Paripuṇṇapinitapahaṭṭhasubbhanīyo hoti. Niyatasantasukhasamāpattiyo hoti.
  Acalasīlapavarāsamasucigandhaparibhāvito hoti. Devamanussānaṃ piyo hoti.
  Khīṇāsavabalavanti hoti. Ariyavarapuggalajinasāsanapatthito hoti. Bhūvī
  ca hoti divī ca devamanussānaṃ. Asurānaṃ vanditapūjito hoti.
  Anagghajanānaṃ dhuvathavitathomitapasaṭṭho hoti. Idha bhūvī ca divī ca
  lokamanupalitto hoti. Appathokānuvajje bhayadassāvī hoti.
  Sampattivaravipulakāmānaṃ maggaphalapavaratthasādhano hoti.
  Āyācitavipulapaṇītapaccayabhāgī hoti. Idamatthitasarīrāyapi aniketasayanakāmī
  hoti. Jhānajjhāyitapavaravihārī hoti. Vijaṭitajālakilesavatthu-
  viddhaṃsitabhaggasaṃkuṭitasañchinnagatinīvaraṇo hoti. Akuppadhamme abhinippavāso
  hoti. Anavajjitabhogī hoti. Gativimutto. Uttiṇṇasabbavicikiccho.
  Vimuttijjhāyitatto. Diṭṭhadhamme acaladaḷhabhīruttāṇamanupagato.
  Anusayasamucchinno. Sabbāsavakkhayappatto. Santasukhasamāpattiṃ paṭilabhati.
  Samaṇaguṇasamupeto hoti. Imehi tiṃsaguṇavarehi samupeto hoti.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 467]
      Nanu mahārāja thero sārīputto dasasahassamhi lokadhātuyā
  aggapuriso ṭhapetvā dasabalaṃ lokuttarācariyaṃ. So hi
  aparimitāsaṃkheyyakappe samācitakusalamūlo brāhmaṇakulakulino hutvā mānusiratiṃ
  atiohāya dhanavaraṃ chaḍḍayitvā jinasāsane pabbajitvā imehi
  terasadhutaṅgaguṇehi kāyavacīcittaṃ damayitvā ajjetarahi anantaguṇasamupeto
  bhagavato sāsane dhammacakkaṃ anupavattako jāto. Bhāsitaṃ cetaṃ
  mahārāja bhagavatā devātidevena ekaṅguttaranikāyavaralañcake
      nāhaṃ bhikkhave aññaṃ ekapuggalampi samanupassāmi yo evaṃ
  tathāgatena anuttaraṃ dhammacakkaṃ pavattitaṃ sammadeva anuppavatteti
  yathayidaṃ bhikkhave sārīputto sārīputto bhikkhave tathāgatena anuttaraṃ
  dhammacakkaṃ pavattitaṃ sammadeva anuppavattetīti. Evaṃ mahārāja
  dhutaṅgaguṇaṃ anantaguṇaṃ paraṃ detīti.
      Sādhu bhante nāgasena yaṃ kiñci navaṅgaṃ buddhavacanaṃ yā ca
  lokuttarā kiriyā yā ca loke adhigamavipulavarasampattiyo sabbantaṃ
  terasasu dhutaṅgaguṇesu samodhānopagatanti.
                     Dhutaṅgapañho navamo.
                       Navamo vaggo.

                        Upamāpañho
      bhante nāgasena katīhaṅgehi samannāgato bhikkhu arahattaṃ
  sacchikarotīti. Idha mahārāja arahattaṃ sacchikattukāmena bhikkhunā
  ghorasarassa gadrabhassa ekaṅgaṃ gahetabbaṃ. Kukkuṭassa pañcaṅgāni

  Makkaṭassa dve aṅgāni gahetabbāni.
