-------------------------------------------------
[page 529]
  Katamāni tāni tīṇaṅgānīti. Yathā mahārāja khettaṃ nāma mātikāya
  sampannaṃ hoti tāya ca pana mātikāya udakaṃ āharitvā khettaṃ
  pavesetvā dhaññaṃ pālenti evameva kho mahārāja yoginā
  yogāvacarena sucaritavattapaṭipattimātikāya sampannena bhavitabbaṃ. Idaṃ
  mahārāja khettassa paṭhamaṅgaṃ gahetabbaṃ. Puna ca paraṃ mahārāja
  khettaṃ mariyādāsampannaṃ hoti tāya ca mariyādāya udakaṃ rakritvā
  dhaññaṃ paripācenti evameva kho mahārāja yoginā yogāvacarena
  sīlamariyādāsampannena bhavitabbaṃ tāya sīlamariyādāya sāmaññaṃ rakkhitvā
  cattāri sāmaññaphalāni gahetabbāni. Idaṃ mahārāja khettassa
  dutiyaṅgaṃ gahetabbaṃ. Puna ca paraṃ mahārāja khettaṃ uṭṭhānasampannaṃ
  hoti sāmikassa hāsajananaṃ appaṃ pi bījaṃ vuttaṃ bahuphalaṃ hoti bahūpi
  vuttaṃ bahutaraṃ hoti evameva kho mahārāja yoginā yogāvacarena
  uṭṭhānasampannena vipulaphaladāyinā bhavitabbaṃ dāyakānaṃ hāsajanakena
  bhavitabbaṃ yathā mahārāja appaṃ dinnaṃ bahuṃ hoti bahuṃ dinnaṃ bahutaraṃ
  hoti. Idaṃ mahārāja khettassa tatiyaṅgaṃ gahetabbaṃ. Bhāsitaṃ
  cetaṃ mahārāja therena upālinā vinayadharena
         khettūpamena bhavitabbaṃ uṭṭhānavipuladāyinā
         esa khettavaro nāma yo dadāti vipulaṃ phalanti.
      Bhante nāgasena agadassa dve aṅgāni gahetabbānīti yaṃ
  vadesi katamāni tāni dve aṅgānīti. Yathā mahārāja agade
-------------------------------------------------
[page 530]
  Kimiyo na saṇṭhahanti evameva kho mahārāja yoginā yogāvacarena
  mānase kilesā na saṇṭhāpitabbā. Idaṃ mahārāja agadassa
  paṭhamaṅgaṃ gahetabbaṃ. Puna ca paraṃ mahārāja agadaṃ daṭṭhuphuṭṭhadiṭṭha-
  asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ sabbaṃ visaṃ paṭihanati evameva kho mahārāja
  yoginā yogāvacarena rāgadosamohamānadiṭṭhivisaṃ paṭihanitabbaṃ. Idaṃ
  mahārāja agadassa dutiyaṅgaṃ gahetabbaṃ. Bhāsitaṃ cetaṃ mahārāja bhagavatā
         saṅkhārānaṃ sabhāvatthaṃ daṭṭhukāmena yoginā
         agadeneva bhavitabbaṃ kilesavisanāsaneti.
      Bhante nāgasena bhojanassa tīṇaṅgāni gahetabbānīti yaṃ vadesi
  katamāni tāni tīṇaṅgānīti. Yathā mahārāja bhojanaṃ nāma
  sabbasattānaṃ upatthambho evameva kho mahārāja yoginā yogāvacarena
  sabbasattānaṃ upatthambhena bhavitabbaṃ. Idaṃ mahārāja bhojanassa
  paṭhamaṅgaṃ gahetabbaṃ. Puna ca paraṃ mahārāja bhojanaṃ sattānaṃ balaṃ
  vaḍḍheti evameva kho mahārāja yoginā yogāvacarena puññavuddhiyā
  vaḍḍhitabbaṃ. Idaṃ mahārāja bhojanassa dutiyaṅgaṃ gahetabbaṃ.
  Puna ca paraṃ mahārāja bhojanaṃ sabbasattānamabhipatthitaṃ evameva kho
  mahārāja yoginā yogāvacarena sabbalokābhipatthitena bhavitabbaṃ.
  Idaṃ mahārāja bhojanassa tatiyaṅgaṃ gahetabbaṃ. Bhāsitaṃ cetaṃ mahārāja
  therena mogharājena
         sakasāmaññaniyamena sīlena paṭipattiyā
         patthitena bhavitabbaṃ sabbalokassa yogināti.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 531]
      Bhante nāgasena issatthassa cattāri aṅgāni gahetabbānīti
  yaṃ vadesi katamāni tāni cattāri aṅgānīti. Yathā mahārāja
  issattho nāma sare pātayanto ubho pāde paṭhaviyaṃ daḷhaṃ
  patiṭṭhapeti jānuṃ avicalaṃ karoti kaṇṇaṃ sandhimhi ṭhapeti kāyaṃ
  paṭipatthambhaṃ karoti dve hatthe sandhiyaṭṭhānaṃ āropeti muṭṭhiṃ pīḷayati
  aṅguliyo nirantaraṃ karoti gīvaṃ paggaṇhāti cakkhumukhāni pidahati
  nimittaṃ ujuṃ karoti hāsamuppādeti vijjhissāmīti evameva kho
  mahārāja yoginā yogāvacarena sīlapaṭhaviyaṃ viriyapāde daḷhaṃ
  patiṭṭhapetabbaṃ khantisoraccaṃ avicalaṃ kātabbaṃ saṃvare cittaṃ ṭhapetabbaṃ
  saṃyamaniyame attānaṃ upanetabbaṃ icchāmucchā pīḷayitabbā yoniso
  manasikārena cittaṃ nirantaraṃ kātabbaṃ viriyaṃ paggahetabbaṃ cha dvārā
  pidahitabbā sati upaṭṭhāpetabbā hāsaṃ uppādetbbaṃ sabbakilese
  ñāṇanārācena vijjhissāmīti. Idaṃ mahārāja issatthassa paṭhamaṅgaṃ
  gahetabbaṃ. Puna ca paraṃ mahārāja issattho āḷakaṃ pariharati
  vaṅkaṃ jimhaṃ kuṭilaṃ nārācaṃ ujukaraṇāya evameva kho mahārāja
  yoginā yogāvacarena imasmiṃ kāye satipaṭṭhānāaḷakaṃ pariharitabbaṃ
  vaṅkaṃ jimhaṃ kuṭilaṃ cittaṃ ujukaraṇāya. Idaṃ mahārāja issatthassa
  dutiyaṅgaṃ gahetabbaṃ. Puna ca paraṃ mahārāja issattho lakkhe
  upāseti evameva kho mahārāja yoginā yogāvacerena imasmiṃ
  kāye upāsitabbaṃ. Kathaṃ mahārāja yoginā yogāvacarena imasmiṃ
  kāye upāsitabbaṃ. Aniccato upāsitabbaṃ dukkhato anattato
-------------------------------------------------
[page 532]
  Rogato gaṇḍato sallato aghato ābādhato parato palokato
  ītito upaddavato bhayato upasaggato calato pabhaṅguto adhuvato
  aleṇato attāṇato asaraṇato asaraṇībhūtato tucchato suññato
  ādīnavato vipariṇāmadhammato asārato bhayamūlato vadhakato sāsavato
  sāsaṅkato saṃkhatato māramissato jātidhammato jarādhammato
  byādhidhammato maraṇadhammato sokadhammato paridevadhammato
  upāyāsadhammatoti. Evaṃ kho mahārāja yoginā yogāvacarena imasmiṃ
  kāye upāsitabbaṃ. Idaṃ mahārāja issatthassa tatiyaṅgaṃ gahetabbaṃ.
  Puna ca paraṃ mahārāja issattho sāyaṃ pātaṃ upāsati evameva
  kho mahārāja yoginā yogāvacarena sāyaṃ pātaṃ ārammaṇe
  upāsitabbaṃ. Idaṃ mahārāja issatthassa catutthaṅgaṃ gahetabbaṃ.
  Bhāsitaṃ cetaṃ mahārāja therena sārīputtena dhammasenāpatinā
         yathā issatthako nāma sāyapātaṃ upāsati
         upāsanaṃ na riñcanto labhati bhattavetanaṃ
         tatheva buddhaputtopi karoti kāyupāsanaṃ
         kāyupāsanaṃ na riñcanto arahattamadhigacchatīti.
                  Issatthassa pañho pañcamo.
                   ------------------
-------------------------------------------------
[page 533]
      Iti chasu kaṇḍesu bāvīsativaggapaṭimaṇḍitesu dvāsaṭṭhiadhikā
  dvesatā ca imasmiṃ potthake āgatā milindapañhā samattā.
  Anāgatā ca pana dvācattāḷisā honti āgatā ca anāgatā ca
  sabbā samodhānetvā catūhi adhikā tisatapañhā honti sabbā va
  milindapañhāti saṃkhyaṃ gacchanti.
      Rañño ca therassa ca pucchāvisajjanāvasāne caturāsītisata-
  sahassayojanabahalā udakapariyantaṃ katvā ayaṃ mahāpaṭhavī chadhā pakampittha
  vijjulatā nicchariṃsu devatā dibbapupphavassaṃ pavassiṃsu mahābrahmā
  sādhukāramakāsi mahāsamuddakucchiyaṃ meghatthanitanigghoso viya mahāghoso
  ahosi. Iti so milindo rājā ca orodhagaṇā ca sirasā
  añjaliṃ paṇāmetvā vandiṃsu.
      Milindo rājā ativiya pamuditahadayo sumathitamānahadayo
  buddhasāsane sāramatino ratanattaye sunikkaṅkho niggumbo nijjaṭo
  hutvā therassa ca guṇesu pabbajjāsupaṭipadāiriyāpathesu ca ativiya
  pasanno vissaṭṭho nirālayo nihatamānadappo uddhaṭadāṭho viya
  bhujagindo evamāha sādhu sādhu bhante nāgasena buddhavisayo pañho
  tayā visajjito imasmiṃ buddhasāsane ṭhapetvā dhammasenāpati-
  sārīputtattheraṃ añño tayāsadiso pañhāvisajjane natthi. Khamatha me
  bhante nāgasena mama accayaṃ. Upāsakaṃ maṃ bhante nāgasena
  dhāretha ajjatagge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatanti.
      Tadā rājā balakāyehi nāgasenattheraṃ payirupāsitvā milindaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 534]
  Nāma mahāvihāraṃ kāretvā therassa niyyādetvā catūhi paccayehi
  koṭisatehi khīṇāsavehi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ nāgasenattheraṃ paricari. Punapi
  therassa paññāya pasīditvā puttassa rajjaṃ niyyādetvā agārasmā
  anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhetvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇīti.
  Tena vuttaṃ
        paññā pasaṭṭhā lokasmiṃ gatā saddhammaṭhītiyā
        paññāya vimatiṃ hantvā santiṃ papponti paṇḍitā
        yasmiṃ khandhe ṭhitā paññā sati yattha anūnakā
        pūjāvisesassa dharo aggo sova anuttaro
        tasmā hi paṇḍito poso sukhaṃ sampassamattano
        paññavantābhipūjeyya cetiyaṃ viya pūjiyanti.
                Milindassa ceva nāgasenattherassa ca
              pañhāpucchāveyyākaraṇappakaraṇaṃ samattaṃ.
-------------------------------------------------
